Additionally, the knowledgeable memory and execution skills supported by our framework are transferable across diverse base models, improving by 8.5% with Kimi-2.6.

</details>


### [36] [Can Induced Emotion Bias LLM Behaviors in Sequential Decision Making?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12631)
*Minh Khoi Ho,Zihao Zhu,Runchuan Zhu,Levina Li,Zhiwen Fan,Zhangyang Wang,Junyuan Hong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文利用爱荷华赌博任务(IGT)结合基于想象的情绪诱导范式，研究了诱导情绪对LLM代理序贯决策的影响。结果发现，与人类不同，诱导情绪平均而言不会显著偏倚LLM的决策动态，但愤怒情绪有条件效应：使LLM对惩罚更不敏感，并在早期阶段降低探索性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM被部署为高风险领域的自主代理，理解可能调节其决策的情境因素变得至关重要。虽然LLM被训练来感知和共鸣用户情绪，但诱导情绪是否会影响它们的序贯决策尚不清楚。

Method: 采用经典心理学范式爱荷华赌博任务(IGT)结合基于想象的"焦虑vs愤怒"情绪诱导程序。首先验证LLM能从上下文中感知强烈、可区分的情绪，且能以类人速度从序贯交互中学习。然后在验证设置下研究诱导情绪对决策的影响。

Result: 1) 与人类不同，诱导情绪平均而言不会显著偏倚LLM代理的决策动态；2) 愤怒情绪有有条件效应：使LLM对不良决策的惩罚更不敏感；3) 在游戏早期阶段，愤怒会降低探索性，将决策锁定在少数选择上。

Conclusion: 诱导情绪对LLM决策的影响与人类行为存在微妙而显著的区别，表现为条件性而非全局性效应。研究为未来LLM代理的情感调节研究提供了工具。

Abstract: As Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed as autonomous agents in high-stakes domains, understanding contextual factors that may modulate their decision-making becomes critical. While LLMs are trained to perceive and resonate with users' emotions, it remains unclear whether induced emotion can influence their sequential decision-making. We investigate this question using the Iowa Gambling Task (IGT), a classic psychological paradigm for studying decision-making under uncertainty, combined with an imagination-based emotion induction procedure. We first validate the feasibility of this paradigm by confirming that LLMs can sense strong, distinguishable emotions from context and that LLM agents can learn from sequential interactions in a human-like pace. With the validated setup, we find that, different from humans, induced emotion does not significantly bias the decision dynamics of LLM agents on average. However, the effects of anger are conditioned: inducing anger makes LLM agents less sensitive to penalties for bad decisions, and in early stages of the game, anger can lower exploration, locking decisions into a few choices early. These findings reveal the subtle yet distinct effects of induced emotion on LLM decision-making compared to human behavior, and provide a tool for future research on affective modulation of LLM agents.

</details>


### [37] [Segregate, Refine, Integrate: Decomposing Multimodal Fusion for Sentiment Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12686)
*Alexios Filippakopoulos,Elias Kallioras,Nikolaos Xiros,Efthymios Georgiou,Alexandros Potamianos*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出SeRIn多模态融合方案，通过分离模态特定表征与跨模态交互的架构先验，在CH-SIMS和CMU-MOSEI上取得SOTA结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态融合需要同时 refining 模态特定信号和建模跨模态交互，但这两个竞争目标通常在相同操作中纠缠在一起，限制了性能。

Method: SeRIn (Segregate, Refine, Integrate)：模态特定表征沿隔离路径进化，各自针对编码器上下文进行refine；专用跨模态路径累积联合进化而不污染单模态流；完整的跨模态交互延迟到最后预测步骤。

Result: 在CH-SIMS和CMU-MOSEI基准上所有指标均取得SOTA结果；消融实验证实结构化交互（而非增加容量）驱动性能提升；门控分析显示在视觉损坏下出现无监督的模态重加权。

Conclusion: 明确分离模态特定refinement与跨模态交互作为架构先验，能有效提升多模态融合性能，且模型能自发学习模态重加权策略。

Abstract: Multimodal fusion must simultaneously refine modality-specific signals and model cross-modal interactions; two competing objectives typically entangled within the same operation. We propose \textbf{SeRIn} (\textbf{Se}gregate, \textbf{R}efine, \textbf{In}tegrate), a multimodal LM fusion scheme that enforces this separation as an architectural prior. Modality-specific representations evolve along isolated pathways, each refined against its respective encoder context, while a dedicated cross-modal pathway accumulates their joint evolution without contaminating unimodal streams. Full cross-modal interaction is deferred to a final prediction step - ablations confirm that structured interactions, not added capacity, drive the gains; gate analysis under visual corruption reveals emergent modality reweighting without explicit supervision. SeRIn achieves state-of-the-art results on CH-SIMS and CMU-MOSEI, improving all metrics on both benchmarks.

</details>


### [38] [From Critic to Confidence: PPO for Language-Based Quantitative Prediction with Confidence Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12687)
*Mehak Dhaliwal,Rasta Tadayon,Andong Hua,Haewon Jeong,Yao Qin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: CARE-PPO是一个将不确定性估计的损失预测与actor-critic PPO微调相结合的强化学习框架，通过置信度对齐奖励函数实现语言定量预测中准确数值估计与可靠置信信号的联合学习，在医疗和金融任务中相较于基于logit和口头表达的基线方法取得更优的置信度校准效果，并在分布外场景和通用指令遵循能力上均表现出泛化优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在定量预测中容易出现幻觉和过度自信的错误，因此不仅需要知道模型的预测结果，还需要判断其预测何时可信，现有方法在置信度估计方面存在不足。

Method: 提出CARE-PPO框架，将损失预测与actor-critic PPO微调相结合，设计置信度对齐奖励函数（定义为预测误差的函数），为actor提供密集的误差感知反馈，同时引导critic学习与预测质量对齐的价值函数；推理时将critic重新用作置信度估计器。

Result: 在医疗和金融两个真实任务、Qwen-3 4B和8B两个模型规模上，CARE-PPO实现了强定量预测性能，产生了比logit基线和口头表达基线显著更好对齐的置信度估计，在多种分布外设置下保持优势，并减少了对通用指令遵循提示的任务特定过拟合。

Conclusion: CARE-PPO框架有效实现了定量预测准确性与置信度估计可靠性的联合提升，利用RL微调的泛化优势超越了监督方法，为LLM在安全关键领域的可信部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: LLMs can perform language-based quantitative prediction from unstructured inputs, but remain susceptible to hallucinations and overconfident errors, making it critical to know not only what a model predicts, but when its predictions can be trusted. We introduce CARE-PPO, a reinforcement learning framework that establishes a connection between loss prediction for uncertainty estimation and actor-critic PPO fine-tuning, enabling joint learning of accurate numerical estimates and reliable confidence signals in language-based quantitative prediction. CARE-PPO uses a Confidence-Aligned Reward for Estimation, defined as a function of prediction error, to provide dense error-aware feedback to the actor while inducing the critic to learn a value function aligned with prediction quality. During inference, we repurpose the critic as a confidence estimator. Across two real-world tasks in healthcare and finance and two Qwen-3 model scales (4B and 8B), CARE-PPO achieves strong quantitative prediction performance, while producing significantly better-aligned confidence estimates through the critic than logit-based and verbalized baselines. These gains persist under realistic out-of-distribution settings across domains, spanning linguistic and domain shifts. Finally, CARE-PPO reduces task-specific overfitting on general instruction-following prompts, consistent with the broader generalization advantages of RL fine-tuning over supervised approaches.

</details>


### [39] [Epistemic Stance Flexibility Probing: Measuring Prompt-Conditioned Register Shift in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12739)
*Binwen Liu,Yilin Ren*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: ESFP是一个行为基准测试，通过对比外部归因和自身归因提示来评估语言模型在争议性声明上是否能够区分并切换知识信念与自我信念的表达，包含104个精心设计的测试项和四个评估维度。对8个前沿模型的测试发现，认知灵活性与模型通用能力大致正交，有27B开源模型超越最强闭源系统，推理优化模型并不始终表现更好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准（准确率、指令遵循、安全性）无法直接评估语言模型在争议性声明上能否区分"专家观点"和"自身立场"并切换相应的认知语域（中立归因 vs. 立场表达）。

Method: 构建ESFP基准，包含104个精心控制的项目，覆盖6个认知类别和5个措辞模板；从四个互补维度评估模型响应：词汇层面的自我归因、表征层面对角色框架的响应性、句子层面使用LLM评审团评估的立场内容密度、跨条件的立场一致性。

Result: 认知灵活性与模型通用能力大致正交；27B开源模型匹配最强闭源系统；某模型家族的旗舰版性能低于其轻量版；推理优化模型并不始终表现出更高灵活性；立场内容密度提供最强信号，而表面词汇标记（如"I think"）的变化可能不伴随实际立场的相应改变。

Conclusion: ESFP衡量的是模型在不断变化的归因条件下调整认知立场的倾向，而非通用能力指标。

Abstract: A language model may be asked either what experts believe about a contested claim or what it believes about the claim itself. A trustworthy conversational agent should distinguish these two requests and respond in different epistemic registers: neutral attribution in the first case and stance expression in the second. Whether such a shift occurs-and whether it occurs coherently-is not directly assessed by existing benchmarks for accuracy, instruction following, or safety. We introduce ESFP, a behavioral benchmark that treats the contrast between externally attributed and self-attributed prompts as the fundamental unit of measurement. ESFP consists of 104 carefully controlled items spanning six epistemic categories and five phrasing templates, and evaluates model responses along four complementary dimensions: lexical self-attribution, representation-level responsiveness to role framing, sentence-level stance content density assessed by an LLM judge panel, and cross-condition stance consistency. Evaluating eight frontier models from five vendors, we find that epistemic flexibility is largely orthogonal to general model capability: a 27B open-weight model matches the strongest proprietary systems, the flagship model of one family underperforms its lightweight counterpart, and reasoning-optimized models do not consistently exhibit higher flexibility. Stance content density provides the strongest signal, while surface-level lexical markers such as 'I think' can change substantially without corresponding changes in expressed stance. We provide item-level bootstrap confidence intervals, weight-sensitivity analyses, and an explicit discussion of the interpretation limits of the composite score. ESFP measures a model's propensity to adapt its epistemic stance under changing attribution conditions, rather than a general competence measure.

</details>


### [40] [The One-Word Census: Answer-Choice Conformity Across 44 Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12796)
*Tapan Parikh*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过"一词普查"（One-Word Census）这一简约工具，对44个语言模型在31个开放式类别（如"说一种树"）中的回答趋同现象进行了系统性分析。研究发现模型之间存在极端趋同（7个类别中某一答案占比超80%），但个体间从众程度差异达四倍以上，且呈现结构化特征：社区调优模型最发散，最新旗舰模型最从众；同一系列内趋同性随代际递增，但最新旗舰模型（Claude、GPT）出现反转。相比人类，模型在20个共享类别中的18个上更集中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前研究多聚焦于模型能力的基准测试，但缺乏对模型在"任意选择"场景下行为模式的系统化度量。现有趋同研究虽已发现模型间的相似性，但缺乏一个简约、低成本、可复现的工具来量化这种趋同的结构和变异。本研究旨在设计这样一个工具，并揭示模型趋同行为的内部结构及其与模型类型、代际的关系。

Method: 设计"一词普查"（One-Word Census）：31个单一轮次的提示，每个提示命名一个有多样有效答案的类别（如"说一种树"），每个模型重复4次，无系统提示。使用精确匹配（归一化分词后）而非嵌入或评判模型进行分析，每个模型成本约1美元。用"答案选择惊喜度"（answer-choice surprisal）评分：平均-log2概率，计算模型答案相对于所有其他模型答案池（留一法）的概率。对排名进行留一族群交叉验证（leave-one-family-out rho=0.985），并与人类类别产出常模进行对比。

Result: 1）极端趋同：31个类别中有7个类别某一答案占比超80%；2）从众程度差异：模型间变异超四倍；3）结构化变异：人格化/社区调优模型最多样化，最新主流旗舰模型最从众（几乎不产生其他模型未给出的答案）；4）代际趋势：Claude、GPT、Qwen、Grok系列内趋同性随代际上升，但最新旗舰级Claude和GPT出现反转（可能反映顶级模型的重新定位）；5）排名稳定性高（留一族群交叉验证rho=0.985）；6）相比人类：20个共享类别中模型在18个上更集中。

Conclusion: "一词普查"是一个低成本、高信息量的工具，能够有效量化语言模型在开放式选择任务中的趋同行为。模型趋同并非均匀分布，而是受模型类型、调优方式和代际显著影响。社区调优模型保持多样性，而最新旗舰模型极度从众，但最新一代旗舰的趋同度反转可能暗示模型开发策略的转变。该工具为理解模型行为多样性、监督模型趋同演化提供了可复现的基准。

Abstract: When a language model must pick one answer from a large space of equally valid options, which does it pick -- and how often is it the same answer every other model picks? Asked to "pick a word -- any word," 44 models chose "serendipity" 41% of the time. We characterize this convergence with a deliberately minimal instrument: 31 single-turn prompts, each naming a category with many valid one-word answers ("Name a tree."), asked four times per model with no system prompt. Analysis is exact-match on normalized tokens -- no embeddings, no judge -- at about a dollar per model. That models converge is well documented; our contribution is the instrument itself -- the One-Word Census -- and what it reveals about the structure of the convergence. We score each model by answer-choice surprisal: the average $-\log2$ probability of its answers under the pooled answers of all other models, leave-one-out. Convergence is extreme -- in 7 of 31 categories one answer takes over 80% of all answers -- yet conformity varies more than fourfold across models, and the variation is structured. Persona- and community-tuned models are the most divergent; the newest mainline flagships are the most conformist, producing almost no answer no other model gave. Within four lineages (Claude, GPT, Qwen, Grok) conformity rises with each generation -- but reverses for the latest flagship Claude and GPT models, a possible early signal of repositioning at the top tier. Rankings are robust to roster composition (leave-one-family-out rho = 0.985). Against human category-production norms, the field is more concentrated than people in 18 of 20 shared categories. All prompts, transcripts, and code are public.

</details>


### [41] [Knowledgeless Language Models: Suppressing Parametric Recall for Evidence-Grounded Language Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12831)
*Roi Cohen,Yvan Carré,Nick Lechtenbörger,Hendrik Droste,Lucas Kerschke,Russa Biswas,Gerard de Melo,Jan Buys*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出知识缺失语言模型（KLLMs），通过在预训练语料中匿名化命名实体来抑制参数化事实记忆，使模型转向基于证据的推理，在上下文问答、事实验证和幻觉检测等任务中显著优于标准模型，且在检索增强场景下取得20-25%的相对提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型在参数中编码了大量事实知识，但这些知识可能过时、不完整或与上下文不一致，导致不可靠行为。现有方法多通过后处理（如检索增强）缓解此问题，但缺乏从预训练阶段系统性控制知识获取的手段。

Method: 提出KLLMs范式，在预训练语料中对命名实体进行匿名化处理，移除实体级事实监督的主要通道。通过对比不同规模的KLLMs与标准语言模型，在多项基准上评估其上下文推理、事实验证、幻觉检测及校准能力。

Result: KLLMs在上下文问答、事实验证和幻觉检测基准上持续优于匹配基线；在检索增强场景下，面对不完美证据时展现出更好的鲁棒性，取得20-25%的相对提升；同时表现出更优的校准性能（ECE、Brier分数、AUROC）和更可靠的弃权行为。

Conclusion: 抑制实体级监督的预训练可以有效诱导模型从参数化知识依赖转向外部证据依赖，提升可靠性。预训练阶段的知识获取控制可作为检索增强和工具使用系统的补充方案，提供更具证据敏感性的基础模型。

Abstract: Language models encode substantial factual knowledge in their parameters, which can lead to unreliable behavior when this knowledge is outdated, incomplete, or misaligned with the provided context. In this work, we study whether modifying the pretraining signal can systematically shift models away from parametric recall and toward evidence-grounded reasoning. We introduce Knowledge--''Less'' Language Models (KLLMs), a fundamentally different epistemic training paradigm for LLMs, which are pretrained on corpora in which named entities are anonymized, thereby removing a primary channel for entity-linked factual supervision. This intervention substantially reduces closed-book factual recall, while often improving performance on tasks where relevant information is provided as context. Across multiple model scales, KLLMs consistently outperform matched baselines on contextual question answering, fact verification, and hallucination detection benchmarks. Crucially, in retrieval-grounded settings with imperfect evidence, KLLMs show improved robustness and achieve up to 20--25\% relative gains over standard language models. They further exhibit better calibration, with improved ECE, Brier score, and AUROC, as well as more reliable abstention behavior. Our results demonstrate that suppressing entity-linked supervision during pretraining induces a shift in epistemic behavior: KLLMs rely less on parametric knowledge and more on external evidence, leading to improved reliability under realistic conditions. This suggests that pretraining-time control over knowledge acquisition can complement retrieval-augmented and tool-based systems by providing a more evidence-sensitive base model.

</details>


### [42] [Can LLMs Write Reliable Rubrics? A Meta-Evaluation for Experiment Reproduction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12835)
*Hanhua Hong,Yizhi Li,Jiaoyan Chen,Luu Gia Huy,Sophia Ananiadou,Jung-jae Kim,Chenghua Lin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文首次系统性地元评估了LLM生成的论文复现评分准则，发现增强设置能显著提升下游评估对齐度（接近人类基线），但生成的准则存在粒度过细、评分偏高和领域适应性不足等问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于评分准则的评估虽能减少幻觉，但构建论文特定准则需要大量专家努力，限制了PaperBench等基准的可扩展性，因此需要探索LLM自动生成评分准则的可行性。

Method: 将评分准则重构为清单格式，在四种生成设置和两种骨干模型下进行评估；通过语义相似性（内在）和与真实准则的分数对齐（外在）进行元评估。

Result: 增强设置显著提升了下游评估对齐度，最强设置接近人类基线，但内在提升幅度较小；LLM生成的准则粒度过细、偏向高分、领域适应性差。

Conclusion: LLM生成评分准则在提升可扩展性方面具有潜力，但存在粒度、偏差和适应性等局限性，需进一步改进。

Abstract: Rubric-based evaluation is a promising approach for assessing open-ended outputs from LLM-based research agents, particularly in paper reproduction, where direct paper-to-repository comparison is prone to hallucination. However, constructing paper-specific rubrics requires substantial expert effort, limiting the scalability of benchmarks such as PaperBench. In this work, we present, to our knowledge, the first systematic meta-evaluation of LLM-generated rubrics for paper reproduction. We reformulate rubrics into a checklist-style format and evaluate four generation settings across two backbone models. We meta-evaluate generated rubrics intrinsically by semantic similarity and extrinsically by score alignment with ground-truth rubrics. Our results show that the augmented settings substantially improves downstream evaluation alignment, with the strongest setting approaching the human baseline, while intrinsic gains are more modest. Further analyses reveal that LLM-generated rubrics are often overly fine-grained, biased toward high scores, and less adaptive to paper domains, highlighting both the affordances and limitations.

</details>


### [43] [Evaluating Large Language Models on Misconceptions in Multi-Turn Medical Conversations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12884)
*Monica Munnangi,Saiph Savage*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了ThReadMed-QA数据集（2,437条医患对话线程，8,204个问答对），用于评估大语言模型在多轮对话中检测和纠正患者错误观念的能力。实验发现，即使GPT-5和Claude-Haiku等前沿模型在单轮交互中纠正错误观念的成功率约85%，但在两轮后续追问后骤降至约50%。性能下降主要源于错误传播，即使提供正确上下文也难以完美维持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估框架无法捕捉LLM在多轮医疗对话中处理错误观念的行为，而患者提问常嵌入错误假设，安全沟通不仅需要回答问题，还需识别并纠正背后的错误信念。LLM能否在长时间对话中可靠地纠正错误观念尚缺乏系统研究。

Method: 构建ThReadMed-QA数据集（来自AskDocs的真实患者交互），采用基于评分标准的LLM-as-a-Judge框架评估五个LLM在多轮对话中识别和纠正错误观念的能力，并进行oracle分析（用医生回复替换模型先前输出）以探究性能下降原因。

Result: 前沿模型在单次交互中纠正错误观念表现良好（~85%），但在两轮后续追问后性能骤降至约50%。性能下降主要由错误传播驱动，即使提供正确上下文，性能仍不完美。

Conclusion: 当前LLM在多轮医疗对话中纠正错误观念的能力存在显著退化，可能导致不一致甚至不安全的患者指导，凸显了建立捕捉多轮行为评估框架的必要性。

Abstract: Patients seeking medical information often ask questions that embed incorrect assumptions or misconceptions. In such cases, safe medical communication requires not only answering the question, but identifying and correcting the underlying false belief. These interactions naturally unfold over multiple turns, a pattern now mirrored in interactions with LLMs. Yet current evaluation frameworks do not capture model behavior in these settings, where misconceptions can emerge, persist, or evolve over the course of a conversation. Whether LLMs can reliably correct such misconceptions over time remains largely unexamined. To study this, we introduce ThReadMed-QA, a multi-turn medical dialogue dataset of 2,437 patient-physician conversation threads comprising 8,204 question-answer pairs, derived from real patient interactions on AskDocs. This dataset enables systematic evaluation of whether models can detect and correct misconceptions under a multi-turn context. We evaluate five LLMs using a rubric-based LLM-as-a-Judge framework that scores responses based on their ability to identify and correct misconceptions. Our experiments reveal a consistent pattern: even frontier models that can address misconceptions in a single interaction degrade substantially over subsequent turns. GPT-5 and Claude-Haiku correct these false presuppositions around 85% on initial questions but drop to roughly 50% within two follow-ups. An oracle analysis replacing prior model outputs with physician responses shows that much of the degradation is driven by error propagation, while performance remains imperfect even under correct context. Even when models tend to correct misconceptions initially, their performance degrades substantially over later turns, leading to inconsistent and potentially unsafe guidance in patient-facing settings and highlighting the need for evaluation frameworks that capture multi-turn behavior.

</details>


### [44] [LLM Judges Can Be Too Generous When There Is No Reference Answer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12885)
*Chalamalasetti Kranti,Sowmya Vajjala*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过校准实验和敏感性实验发现，LLM裁判在无参考答案设置下倾向于高估错误答案，而引入参考答案可显著改变裁判判断（最高达85%），且这些变化与人类判断一致，因此建议在使用无参考评估前先用有参考样本进行校准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究LLM裁判在无参考（no-reference）设定下评估开放生成回答的可靠性，因为该设定中缺乏标准答案，裁判模型是否可信尚不明确。

Method: 采用两阶段流水线：a) 校准实验——评估裁判模型对评估任务的认知水平；b) 敏感性实验——考察参考答案是否存在及其在提示中的位置如何影响裁判模型的表现。实验覆盖三种语言。

Result: 1) 无参考答案时，裁判模型倾向于高估错误回答；2) 在提示中添加参考答案可使裁判的正确/错误判断发生高达85%的翻转；3) 这些参考答案驱动的判断变化与人类评估结果基本一致。

Conclusion: 在可靠地使用无参考评估之前，必须先用有参考样本对LLM裁判进行校准；本文提供了一套可供研究人员和实践者参考的校准方法论蓝图。

Abstract: LLM judges are increasingly being used to evaluate open-ended model responses, often in no-reference settings where a ground-truth answer is unavailable. However, can they reliably assess in such evaluation setups? We explore this question in this paper through a two stage pipeline with a) calibration experiments that assess the judge model's knowledge of the task it is evaluating, and b) sensitivity experiments that assess how the judge model's performance is impacted by the presence and positioning of the reference answer in the prompt. Across experiments covering three languages, we show that the judge models we evaluated tend to over-credit incorrect answers in the absence of a reference answer, and adding reference answer information to the prompt flips the judge model's correct/incorrect decisions by as much as 85% in some experimental settings. Comparison with a subset of human annotations shows that these reference-driven changes generally align with human judgments. Our results emphasize the need for calibrating the LLM judges with a sample with reference-aware evaluation before using them in reference-free setups reliably, and our methodology provides a blueprint for researchers and practitioners in doing such calibration of LLM judges for other tasks.

</details>


### [45] [The Illusion of Robustness: Aggregate Accuracy Hides Prediction Flips under Task-Irrelevant Context](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12963)
*Yanzhe Zhang,Sanmi Koyejo,Diyi Yang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 大型语言模型在长上下文任务中看似鲁棒，但实际存在显著的单样本不稳定性：无关上下文（包括随机伪词）会双向影响模型预测，这种不稳定受上下文类型、长度、测试时计算和模型开发阶段调节，揭示了聚合准确率掩盖的尾部风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM能力提升并被部署到上下文丰富的场景中，任务输入常伴随长而部分无关的上下文。现有评估主要依赖聚合准确率，但该方法可能掩盖模型在单个样本上的不稳定性，需要深入探究无关上下文对模型预测的微观影响。

Method: 在受控设置下，向基准问题前添加无关上下文（包括随机组合字符形成的伪词），测量SOTA模型在聚合层面和单样本层面的准确率变化，分析上下文类型、长度、测试时计算和模型开发阶段等因素对不稳定性的调节作用。

Result: 聚合层面模型对无关上下文表现鲁棒，但单样本层面存在显著不稳定。即使是无意义的伪词也能在少数样本上大幅改变预测，双向影响（既降低又提升性能）。受影响样本因模型而异，且不稳定性受上下文类型、长度、测试时计算和模型开发阶段调控。

Conclusion: 聚合准确率掩盖了上下文诱导的尾部风险，LLM的实际可靠性需要细粒度到单样本级别的评估。

Abstract: As large language models (LLMs) grow more capable, they are increasingly deployed in context-rich settings where task inputs are often accompanied by long, partially irrelevant context. In a controlled setting, we find that state-of-the-art models often appear robust to task-irrelevant context at the aggregate level: prepending it to benchmark questions causes little change in overall accuracy. This aggregate stability, however, masks significant per-example instability. Even semantically meaningless pseudo-words, formed by randomly combining characters, can markedly shift model predictions on a small fraction of examples, degrading performance on some while improving it on others. This two-sided effect holds consistently across a wide range of models and datasets, yet the affected examples are largely model-specific. We further show that this instability is modulated by context type, context length, test-time compute, and model development stage. Together, our findings reveal context-induced tail risks concealed by aggregate accuracy, motivating per-example reliability evaluation of language models.

</details>


### [46] [PalmClaw: A Native On-Device Agent Framework for Mobile Phones](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13027)
*Hongru Cai,Yongqi Li,Ran Wei,Wenjie Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: PalmClaw是一个原生运行在移动设备上的开源LLM智能体框架，通过将设备能力暴露为带有明确参数、结构化结果和执行边界的设备工具，相比最强基线实现了11.5%的任务成功率提升和94.9%的完成时间缩减。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有移动智能体大多通过GUI操作（点击、滑动、输入）控制手机，形成冗长且依赖界面的操作序列，无法直接访问设备能力，执行边界难以界定。作者希望构建一个原生运行在手机上的智能体框架，直接暴露设备能力为工具，使执行过程明确可控。

Method: PalmClaw框架原生运行于移动设备上，将设备能力（如传感器、数据、应用等）封装为带有显式参数、结构化结果和清晰执行边界的"设备工具"，在设备本地管理会话、记忆、技能、工具和智能体循环。

Result: 相比最强基线，任务成功率相对提升11.5%，完成时间减少94.9%，且设置负担更低，并展示了执行边界的应用轨迹。

Conclusion: PalmClaw通过设备工具化设计，使LLM智能体能够直接、高效且可控地调用移动设备原生能力，大幅优于依赖GUI操作的现有方法。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM) agents have moved beyond generating responses to executing multi-step tasks by calling tools, observing the results, and iteratively deciding the next action. Most agent systems run on desktops or servers, which support tool use and task automation. Mobile devices are also important agent environments because they are widely accessible and contain users' data, sensors, and daily-use applications. Existing mobile agents mainly operate smartphones through graphical user interface (GUI) actions such as tapping, swiping, and typing, which often form long, interface-dependent sequences, cannot directly access device capabilities, and make execution boundaries difficult to define. We present \textbf{PalmClaw}, an open-source agent framework that runs natively on mobile phones and manages the sessions, memory, skills, tools, and agent loop directly on the device. PalmClaw exposes device capabilities as device tools with explicit arguments, structured results, and clearly defined execution boundaries. This design enables agents to use mobile capabilities directly while keeping each action explicit and controlled. Experiments show an 11.5\% relative improvement in task success and a 94.9\% reduction in completion time over the strongest baseline, with lower setup burden and traces illustrating how execution boundaries are applied. Code is available at https://github.com/ModalityDance/PalmClaw.

</details>

### [47] [HARP-ME: Closure-Driven Exact Induced Motif Enumeration on GPUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12074)
*Ashwina Kumar,Rupesh Nasre*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: HARP-ME是一个GPU框架，通过闭包感知编译、诱导签名复用和规范锚点所有者规则，实现对连接的四节点诱导子图的高效精确枚举，在多种图上比现有方法快1.83-10.73倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GPU上精确诱导子图枚举面临三个挑战：候选扩展不规则、重复集合交集主导执行、诱导计数需同时考虑边的存在与缺失。现有方法效率不足。

Method: 提出了闭包感知编译（综合考虑遍历代价、代数推导收益、状态复用及图分区光环开销来选择锚点）、诱导签名复用（利用候选前沿信息和紧凑约束识别可复用的完成状态）、以及跨分区的规范锚点所有者规则。

Result: 在六种图上，HARP-ME比Pangolin快2.11倍，比分区PBE快1.83倍，比CPU基线快10.73倍；缓存命中率64%-76%，主机到设备数据传输开销显著低于PBE风格分区。

Conclusion: 优化锚点选择以获取代数推导收益，可以补充基于遍历和基于复用的GPU子图枚举技术。

Abstract: Exact induced motif enumeration is a fundamental operation in graph mining, but it remains challenging on GPUs because candidate expansion is irregular, repeated set intersections dominate execution, and induced counting must consider both the presence and absence of edges. We present HARP-ME, which stands for Hierarchical Anchor-Reuse Partitioned Motif Enumeration. It is a GPU framework for the exact enumeration of connected induced four-node motifs. HARP-ME introduces closure-aware compilation, which selects a set of anchors for explicit enumeration by considering traversal cost, algebraic derivation benefits, expected state reuse, and the additional halo overhead caused by graph partitioning. HARP-ME also introduces induced-signature reuse. This technique identifies reusable completion states using candidate-frontier information together with compact constraints representing both adjacency and non-adjacency relationships. For graphs that exceed GPU memory, a canonical anchor-owner rule ensures exact counting across partitions with overlapping halo regions. We do not claim that the individual graphlet closure identities are new. Instead, our contribution is their integration into a GPU execution framework that reduces both explicit candidate expansion and repeated induced-edge checks. Across six social, web, biological, and synthetic graphs, HARP-ME is the fastest among the evaluated methods. It achieves up to 2.11 times speedup over Pangolin, up to 1.83 times speedup over partitioned PBE, and up to 10.73 times speedup over the evaluated CPU baseline. Detailed measurements show cache-hit rates between 64% and 76%, along with substantially lower host-to-device data transfer overhead than PBE-style partitioning. These results demonstrate that optimizing anchor selection for algebraic derivation benefits can complement traversal-based and reuse-based GPU motif enumeration techniques.

</details>

*Rafael Ferreira da Silva,Milad Abolhasani,Peter Beaucage,Laura Biven,Michael Bussmann,Kyle Chard,Ryan Coffee,Stephen DeWitt,Sagar Dolas,Carrie Eckert,David Elbert,Ian Foster,Tirthankar Ghosal,Anna Giannakou,Tom Gibbs,Leslie Hamilton,Glenn Lockwood,Theresa Mayer,Ben Mintz,Raffi Nazikian,Sal Nimer,Amanda Randles,Woong Shin,Sreenivas Rangan Sukumar,Frédéric Suter,Mitra Taheri,Michela Taufer,Draguna Vrabie*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文更新了自主科学实验室的路线图，指出当前领域面临的核心矛盾：产生候选发现已不再困难，但验证却成为瓶颈。原五大维度扩展为七大维度，将信任/验证/可重复性和安全/安全/治理提升为一级维度，新增四个里程碑，规划了两年发展路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主实验室领域在过去一年发展远超预期，但同时也出现了虚假引用、发现结果被修正等问题。产生发现与验证发现之间的不对称已取代模型能力成为领域的主要限制因素，因此需要更新路线图以反映这一核心张力。

Method: 基于AISLE原始路线图的五大维度框架，评估14个原始里程碑的完成状态（已完成、部分完成、重构或未完成），新增4个里程碑，将两个跨领域关切提升为一级维度，规划两年分阶段实施路径。

Result: 多智能体系统已产出实验验证的假设，自驱实验室互操作性提升，推理训练和领域基础模型能力增强，Genesis任务将自主实验置于美国联邦科学战略核心。同时，旗舰发现结果被修正，基准测试显示智能体仅能完成部分开放式研究任务，顶级会议出现伪造引用。

Conclusion: 领域已从"能否产生发现"转向"能否验证发现"的新阶段。第一年聚焦接口、协议采用和验证框架搭建，第二年瞄准联邦化、零信任协调和治理。草根网络应作为互操作性基础设施，连接国家计划、国际倡议和商业平台，防止再次孤岛化。

Abstract: One year ago, the AISLE roadmap argued that autonomous laboratories operated as isolated islands and proposed a grassroots network organized around five critical dimensions. The field has since moved faster than anticipated. Multi-agent systems have produced experimentally validated hypotheses, self-driving laboratories have grown more interoperable and orchestrated, reasoning-trained and domain foundation models have raised the capability ceiling, and the Genesis Mission has placed autonomous experimentation at the center of U.S. federal science strategy, with industry emerging as a primary actor. Progress has met a sobering counter-current, including a corrected flagship discovery result, benchmarks showing that agents which rival experts on closed-ended questions still complete only a fraction of open-ended research, and fabricated citations surfacing at leading venues. We read this as the defining tension of the field. Producing a candidate discovery is no longer the hard part, but verifying it is, and this asymmetry now limits autonomous science more than raw model capability. We update the roadmap around seven dimensions, revisiting the original five and elevating two former cross-cutting concerns, trust, verification, and reproducibility, and safety, security, and governance, to first-class status. We assess the original milestones (M1 through M14) as achieved, partially achieved, reframed, or open, add four new milestones (M15 through M18), and scope the path forward to a two-year horizon. The first year concentrates on interfaces, protocol adoption, and the scaffolding of verification, and the second targets federation, zero-trust coordination, and governance. Throughout, we position the grassroots network as the interoperability fabric that lets national programs, international initiatives, and commercial platforms connect rather than re-silo.

</details>


### [49] [FlashDiff: Efficient Regional Execution and Scheduling for Diffusion Model Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12121)
*Yaqi Qiao,Ping He,Songrun Xie,Ayush Barik,Chensong Zhang,Zhengzhong Tu,Fan Lai*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: FlashDiff是一个扩散模型服务系统，通过自适应区域执行和调度机制，利用扩散过程在潜空间不同区域和去噪步骤中的非均匀性，选择性执行需要进一步细化的区域，并将节省的计算资源重新分配给并发请求，从而降低30-97%的服务延迟并提升1.2-2.2倍的吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型在图像、视频和音频生成中已成为核心框架，但其服务效率面临挑战。扩散推理需要对高维潜变量进行多步去噪，这种全区域执行模式导致生成延迟高、服务吞吐受限。现有的多GPU并行化方法虽能减少每步计算，但引入大量激活交换开销，通信成本甚至抵消了并行执行带来的收益。

Method: FlashDiff包含三个核心机制：(1) 利用早期注意力信号将潜表示分解为连贯的执行区域，保留语义结构的同时暴露细粒度并行性；(2) 使用轻量级运行时控制器估计区域活跃度，跳过低影响区域的后续更新；(3) 应用亲和性感知在线调度器，共置依赖区域、平衡GPU负载并重用释放的计算容量。

Result: 在真实的图像、视频和音频工作负载上，FlashDiff将端到端服务延迟降低30-97%，吞吐量提升1.2-2.2倍。

Conclusion: FlashDiff通过自适应区域执行和调度显著提升了扩散模型的服务效率，验证了利用扩散过程中区域非均匀性和步骤间时间相关性来跳过冗余计算的有效性。

Abstract: Diffusion models have become the central backbone for modern image, video, and audio generation, but their efficient service remains a challenge. Unlike autoregressive decoding, diffusion inference repeatedly updates high-dimensional spatial or temporal latents over many denoising steps. This all-region execution pattern makes generation latency high and limits serving throughput. Existing multi-GPU parallelization methods can reduce per-step computation, but often introduce substantial activation exchange overhead, causing communication to offset or even outweigh the benefits of parallel execution.
  This paper presents FlashDiff, a diffusion serving system that improves inference efficiency through adaptive regional execution and scheduling. FlashDiff is based on the observation that diffusion refinement is not uniform across latent regions or denoising steps: different regions often stabilize at different rates, while neighboring steps exhibit strong temporal correlation. FlashDiff leverages these properties to selectively execute only regions that require further refinement and to reallocate the resulting compute slack across concurrent serving requests. FlashDiff consists of three mechanisms. First, it decomposes the latent representation into coherent execution regions using early-stage attention signals, preserving semantic structure while exposing fine-grained parallelism. Second, it uses a lightweight runtime controller to estimate region activity and bypass low-impact updates when further refinement is unlikely to affect output quality. Third, it applies an affinity-aware online scheduler that co-locates dependent regions, balances residual load across GPUs, and reuses reclaimed compute capacity to improve serving efficiency. Across real-world image, video, and audio workloads, FlashDiff reduces end-to-end serving latency by 30-97% and improves throughput by 1.2-2.2x.

</details>


### [50] [Decentralized Gradient Descent: Bottleneck Regimes and Budget Complexity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12172)
*Nicolò Michelusi*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文从资源感知角度研究去中心化梯度下降（DGD）算法，提出了瓶颈中心框架，通过引入梯度多样性-网络连接比（DNR）和梯度-通信噪声比（GCR）两个关键量，刻画了在不同误差尺度下的优化瓶颈，并推导了最优步长选择和预算复杂度界限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管DGD的收敛性质已被充分理解，但达到指定精度所需的通信和计算资源尚不明确，因此需要从资源感知角度系统分析DGD的通信-计算预算。

Method: 提出瓶颈中心框架，区分初始化、目标异质性与网络连接、梯度噪声、通信噪声主导的运行区间；引入DNR和GCR两个关键量；采用多阶段分析推导最优步长和预算复杂度界限。

Result: 得到了描述不同瓶颈序列下的预算最优策略，以及将总预算分解为连续瓶颈贡献的解析表达式。

Conclusion: 揭示了目标异质性、网络连接性、梯度噪声和通信噪声之间的基本权衡关系。

Abstract: Decentralized gradient descent (DGD) is widely used for solving distributed optimization problems over networks of agents. While its convergence properties are well understood, less is known about the communication and computation resources required to attain a prescribed accuracy. In this paper, we study DGD from a resource-aware perspective and characterize the communication-computation budget required to attain a target error level. We develop a bottleneck-centric framework in which different factors dominate the optimization dynamics at different error scales. Specifically, we identify operating regimes governed by initialization, objective heterogeneity and network connectivity, gradient noise, and communication noise. To capture these effects, we introduce two fundamental quantities: the gradient-Diversity-to-Network-connectivity Ratio (DNR) and the Gradient-to-Communication-noise Ratio (GCR). We show that these quantities determine the sequence of bottlenecks encountered during optimization and the corresponding budget-optimal operating strategy. Using a multi-stage analysis, we derive optimal stepsize selections and explicit budget-complexity bounds that quantify the budget resources required to attain a prescribed accuracy. The resulting expressions reveal how the overall budget decomposes into contributions associated with successive bottlenecks and provide insight into the fundamental tradeoffs among objective heterogeneity, network connectivity, gradient noise, and communication noise.

</details>


### [51] [Overcoming Orchestration Bottlenecks at Exascale: A Decentralized, Policy-Driven Approach for Sim-AI Ensembles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12211)
*Harikrishna Tummalapalli,Christine M. Simpson,Riccardo Balin,Vitali A. Morozov,Thang D. Pham,Murat Keceli,Thomas D. Uram*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出EnsembleLauncher，一种递归分层的工作流编排器，采用完全去中心化控制平面和可编程调度策略接口，在Aurora超级计算机上成功扩展到整个系统，支持高达800万串行任务，性能比现有工具提升4倍以上，并展示了调度策略对高方差集成和主动学习管线的资源利用率有显著影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学计算正从单体应用转向耦合仿真-AI工作流，包含异构任务且规模极大，现有系统级调度器吞吐量有限，工作流工具也因刚性控制平面拓扑和静态调度启发式方法面临可扩展性瓶颈。

Method: 提出递归分层的工作流编排架构，核心包括完全去中心化的控制平面和可编程调度策略接口，以应对极端集成规模和任务变异性带来的编排瓶颈。

Result: 在Aurora超级计算机上成功扩展到整个机器，支持800万串行任务，性能优于现有工具4倍以上；可编程调度接口显著影响高方差集成和主动学习管线的资源利用率。

Conclusion: EnsembleLauncher通过去中心化控制和可编程调度策略，有效解决了大规模耦合仿真-AI工作流的编排瓶颈，为未来超算工作流管理提供了新范式。

Abstract: Scientific computing is increasingly shifting from monolithic applications to coupled simulation-AI workflows composed of highly heterogeneous tasks with diverse hardware, scale, and runtime requirements. As these workflows scale to leadership-class systems, the resulting extreme ensemble sizes and task variability can create orchestration bottlenecks. System-level schedulers are often configured for limited throughput, while workflow tools face scalability issues due to rigid control-plane topologies and static scheduling heuristics. We introduce EnsembleLauncher, a recursively hierarchical workflow orchestrator for exascale systems, featuring a fully decentralized control plane and a programmable scheduling policy interface. On the Aurora supercomputer, EnsembleLauncher successfully scales to the entire machine with up to eight million serial tasks, outperforming state-of-the-art tools by more than four times. Additionally, we implement a programmable scheduling interface and demonstrate a significant impact of scheduling policies on resource utilization for high-variance ensembles and active learning pipelines representative of modern coupled simulation-AI workflows.

</details>


### [52] [Profiling and Scheduling Complex O-RAN Applications Across the 5G Edge and Cloud](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12230)
*Yoonjae Hwang,Bhaskar Krishnamachari*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: O-DAG是一个端到端框架，通过DAGProfiler性能分析、三层次网络拓扑建模、SAGA调度扩展和MintEDGE仿真四个阶段，实现了O-RAN中AI/ML流水线（DAG）的执行成本分析、异构资源调度和5G环境下验证，实验表明HEFT算法在多数配置下性能最优，且调度排名与工作负载相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: O-RAN架构将智能RAN控制分解为多个相互依赖的AI/ML功能流水线（如流量预测、信号质量估计、切片调度），这些流水线需在跨远边缘、近边缘和云资源的分散连续体上执行，受异构延迟和带宽约束。然而，目前尚无集成方法论来对其执行成本进行分析、通过调度启发式将其映射到分散基础设施，并在5G蜂窝条件下验证放置结果。

Method: 提出O-DAG框架，包含四个紧密耦合阶段：(1) DagProfiler——开源工具，对O-RAN切片调度器进行插桩并提取每任务指令计数和每边通信量；(2) 参数化三层次网络拓扑，编码远边缘(DU, RIC)、近边缘(edge)和云节点及真实链路带宽；(3) 扩展SAGA调度框架；(4) 基于MintEDGE仿真器的自定义DAG仿真模块。评估了HEFT、MCT、MinMin、MaxMin、Duplex五种调度算法。

Result: HEFT在所有配置下实现最低makespan，但调度器排名与工作负载相关。SAGA-仿真间隙可作为状态诊断指标：负间隙（最高-1.72%）标识计算主导配置（HEFT保守高估），正间隙（+0.64%）在低切片数时标识通信受限状态（带宽争用超出调度模型假设）。

Conclusion: O-DAG填补了O-RAN AI/ML流水线端到端分析、调度和验证的空白，提供了完整的工具链和基准评估。所有工件已开源以供可重复性验证。

Abstract: The O-RAN paradigm decomposes intelligent RAN control into pipelines of interdependent AI/ML functions, including traffic prediction, signal quality estimation, and slice scheduling, that must execute across a dispersed continuum of far-edge, near-edge, and cloud resources under heterogeneous latency and bandwidth constraints. Despite the natural expression of these pipelines as Directed Acyclic Graphs (DAGs), no integrated methodology exists to profile their execution costs, map them onto dispersed infrastructure via scheduling heuristics, and validate the resulting placement under 5G cellular conditions. We present O-DAG, an end-to-end framework that closes this gap through four tightly coupled stages: (1) DagProfiler, a new open-source tool that instruments O-RAN Slice Scheduler and extracts per-task instruction counts and per-edge communication volumes; (2) a parameterized three-tier network topology encoding far-edge (DU, RIC), near-edge (edge), and cloud nodes with realistic link bandwidths; (3) an extension of the SAGA scheduling framework and (4) a custom DAG simulation module built on the MintEDGE simulator. We evaluate five scheduling algorithms (HEFT, MCT, MinMin, MaxMin, Duplex) for a slice scheduling application across various configurations spanning 5K--50K UEs, 2--20 cells, and 2--10 network slices. HEFT achieves the lowest makespan in all configurations, but scheduler rankings are workload-dependent. The SAGA--simulation gap serves as a regime diagnostic: negative gaps (up to -1.72%) identify compute-dominated configurations where HEFT overestimates conservatively, while a positive gap (+0.64%) at low slice counts exposes a communication-bound regime where bandwidth contention exceeds the scheduling model's assumptions. All artifacts are released for reproducibility.

</details>


### [53] [Parallelizing Legacy Mesh Generation Software: Lessons Learned from a Pseudo-Constrained Parallel Data Refinement Approach for Advancing Front Local Reconnection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12253)
*Kevin Garner,David Marcum,Nikos Chrisochoides*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文总结了将遗留软件AFLR作为黑盒进行并行化的经验。采用数据分解和运行时负载均衡系统，在16核时获得约11倍加速，但输出网格质量与串行方法存在差异，且受黑盒输入边界约束限制，可扩展性不佳。作者认为直接并行化这类黑盒遗留代码可能不实用，建议采用原始设计无此类约束的方法以充分利用大规模并行架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索将遗留的AFLR网格生成软件作为黑盒进行并行化的可行性，以利用现代多核架构提升性能，同时评估这种黑盒并行化方法的实际效果与局限性。

Method: 采用数据分解方案，将每个子域在并行环境下使用串行网格生成代码进行细化，并引入运行时系统进行工作负载均衡。将AFLR作为黑盒处理，不修改其内部逻辑。

Result: 并行方法在网格细化操作中稳定良好，16个CPU核心时性能约为串行AFLR的11倍。但受黑盒输入边界要求的约束，并行方法产生的输出网格体积与串行方法不同，且完全稳定性（即生成与串行方法相同质量的网格）和可扩展性受限。

Conclusion: 将AFLR这类黑盒遗留代码并行化可能不切实际，建议采用原始设计即无输入边界约束的方法，以便高效利用大规模并行架构的并发能力。要消除这些约束需要对该先进代码进行非常复杂的修改。

Abstract: This paper presents lessons learned from parallelizing the legacy software known as Advancing Front Local Reconnection (AFLR) as a black box. The parallel procedure utilizes (i) a data decomposition scheme where each subdomain is refined in parallel using the sequential mesh generation code and (ii) a runtime system for work-load balancing. Results on the mesh refinement operation show that the parallel method's stability (output mesh quality) is good and that the parallel method outperforms the serial AFLR by about 11 times when utilizing 16 CPU cores. However, full stability (i.e., generating the same quality as the serial method) and potential scalability is limited due to the constraints set by the black box's input boundary requirement. Satisfying this requirement for each subdomain not only adds overhead but also causes the parallel method to generate a different output mesh volume than that generated by the serial method. The complexity of such a state-of-the-art code requires that it be modified to a non-trivial extent in order to remove these constraints. These results suggest that the parallelization of black-box legacy codes like AFLR may not be practical and instead encourages an approach that is originally designed without such constraints to efficiently leverage the concurrency offered by large-scale architectures.

</details>


### [54] [HeteroMosaic: Exposing and Exploiting Heterogeneous Execution Opportunities for Energy-Efficient Edge LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12839)
*Gregory Hyegang Jun,Wesley Pang,Eddie Richter,Mehdi Saeedi,Aporva Amarnath,Pallavi Ferrao,Deming Chen*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: HeteroMosaic是一个面向边缘SoC异构加速器（iGPU+NPU）的LLM推理调度框架，通过异构Roofline模型、依赖保留的微批次分解和轨迹引导的协同优化，在统一内存平台上实现了最高2.05X加速和45.3%能耗降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM运行时在边缘SoC（CPU+iGPU+NPU）上仅做粗粒度的设备级决策或独立优化算子，未能充分利用异构加速器资源，尤其是在统一内存平台上，性能同时依赖于设备放置和任务图协调。

Method: 1) 异构Roofline模型判断何时联合iGPU与NPU执行有利；2) 将推理分解为保持依赖关系的微批次，暴露跨加速器重叠机会；3) 轨迹引导的调度与设备分配协同优化，考虑内存争用、DVFS、设备差异和NPU运行时开销等实际效应。

Result: 在平衡平台（AMD Ryzen AI）上，相比iGPU基线提速1.73X，NPU基线提速1.78X，llama.cpp等框架提速2.05X，能耗降低45.3%；相比现有异构边缘AI方案提速2.35X。

Conclusion: HeteroMosaic证明了在边缘SoC上通过异构性优先的调度和协同优化，可以显著提升LLM推理性能和能效，为统一内存异构平台提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Modern edge system-on-chips (SoCs) combine CPUs, integrated GPUs (iGPUs), and neural processing units (NPUs), yet existing LLM runtimes typically make coarse device-level decisions or optimize operators in isolation. As a result, they underutilize heterogeneous resources, particularly on unified-memory platforms where performance depends on both device placement and task-graph coordination.
  We present HeteroMosaic, a heterogeneity-first scheduling framework for edge LLM inference. HeteroMosaic first uses a heterogeneous roofline model to identify when combining iGPU and NPU execution is beneficial. It then decomposes inference into dependency-preserving micro-batches that expose cross-accelerator overlap and applies trace-guided co-optimization of scheduling and device allocation under practical effects such as memory contention, DVFS, device variation, and NPU runtime overheads.
  We implement HeteroMosaic in PyTorch C++ and evaluate it on three AMD Ryzen AI platforms spanning NPU-heavy, balanced, and iGPU-heavy designs. On the balanced platform, HeteroMosaic achieves up to 1.73X speedup over an iGPU baseline, 1.78X over an NPU baseline, and 2.05X over frameworks such as \texttt{llama.cpp}, while reducing energy by up to 45.3%. It also improves performance over prior heterogeneous edge AI solutions by up to 2.35X.

</details>


### [55] [Scaling Synthetic-Image Pre-Training for Federated Fine-Tuning of Large Vision Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12583)
*Qianpiao Ma,Xiaozhu Song,Junlong Zhou,Yue Zeng,Jianchun Liu,Huaqing Tu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: FeDiSyn是一个统一框架，通过联合优化预训练与联邦微调，解决大视觉模型在分布式设备上部署面临的资源约束、系统异构性和非IID数据三大挑战，实现训练时间缩短52.5%以上、通信成本降低97.2%以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有联邦微调方法存在三方面不足：合成图像生成方法无法捕获设备特定特征分布；基于PEFT的FedFT方法常低估可能提供关键信息的弱设备；预训练和FedFT的分离优化忽视了二者内在联系，缺乏整体视角来最大化训练效率。

Method: 提出FeDiSyn统一框架，包含三个核心组件：(i) FedFT预训练的缩放定律，用于确定最优合成图像数量；(ii) 基于扩散模型的合成图像生成方法，捕获设备特定特征分布以应对非IID数据；(iii) 贡献感知的LoRA配置与带宽分配算法，确保信息量大的设备被有效利用同时应对系统异构性。

Result: 在真实测试床上，FeDiSyn将完成时间减少52.5%以上，通信成本降低97.2%以上，同时实现与最先进解决方案相当的准确率。

Conclusion: FeDiSyn通过全流程联合优化，有效解决了资源约束、系统异构性和非IID数据三大挑战，为LVM在分布式隐私敏感设备上的联邦微调提供了高效可行的方案。

Abstract: Federated fine-tuning (FedFT) enables adapting pre-trained large vision models (LVMs) on distributed, privacy-sensitive devices, while its practical deployment is hindered by three critical challenges: resource constraints, system heterogeneity, and non-IID data. While prior studies partially address these issues, e.g., by pre-training initial models on synthetic images to mitigate the adverse effects of non-IID data, or leveraging parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) methods like low-rank adaptation (LoRA) to reduce resource consumption, they remain inadequate and fragmented. Specifically, existing synthetic image generation methods fail to capture device-specific feature distributions, while current PEFT-based FedFT methods often undervalue weaker devices that may provide critical information. More importantly, the separate optimization of pre-training and FedFT neglects their inherent connection, lacking a holistic perspective to maximize training efficiency. To overcome these limitations, we propose FeDiSyn, a unified framework that holistically considers the interplay between pre-training and FedFT to minimize the overall LVM training time. Specifically, FeDiSyn introduces: (i) a scaling law for FedFT pre-training to determine the optimal number of synthetic images, balancing pre-training benefit against generation/pre-training cost, (ii) diffusion-based synthetic image generation that captures device-specific feature distributions for pre-training to tackle non-IID data, and (iii) a contribution-aware LoRA configuration and bandwidth allocation algorithm for FedFT to ensure that informative devices are effectively utilized while addressing system heterogeneity. Experimental results on the real-world testbed demonstrate that FeDiSyn reduces completion time by over 52.5% and communication cost by over 97.2%, while achieving comparable accuracy to state-of-the-art solutions.

</details>


### [56] [Proceedings of HLPP 2026: 19th International Symposium on High-Level Parallel Programming and Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12917)
*Chong Li,Corinne Ancourt,Gaétan Hains*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文是HLPP 2026国际研讨会论文集的前言，介绍了2026年7月9-10日在法国巴黎亨利·庞加莱研究所举办的第19届高性能并行编程与应用国际研讨会。会议涵盖并行编程模型、语言、库、算法骨架、编译器和运行时系统，2026届扩展了人工智能相关主题，包括AI系统的并行编程与性能，以及AI辅助并行代码生成。共收录10篇经过单盲评审的论文。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推动高层面并行编程方法的发展，特别是在多核、GPU和分布式平台上，并拓展至人工智能领域的并行编程与性能优化。

Method: 举办国际研讨会，征集并经过单盲同行评审（每篇论文3位程序委员会成员评审）选出10篇论文，在研讨会中展示和交流。

Result: 共10篇经同行评审的论文被收录，按研讨会日程顺序编排。

Conclusion: HLPP 2026成功扩展了传统并行编程范围至AI领域，反映了并行计算与人工智能交叉融合的研究趋势。

Abstract: This volume contains the ten peer-reviewed papers presented at HLPP 2026, the 19th International Symposium on High-Level Parallel Programming and Applications, held on 9-10 July 2026 at the Institut Henri Poincare in Paris, France. The symposium covers high-level approaches to parallel programming: programming models, languages, libraries, algorithmic skeletons, compilers, and runtime systems for multi-core, GPU, and distributed platforms. The 2026 edition extended this scope towards artificial intelligence, with new topics on the parallel programming and performance of AI systems and on AI-assisted generation of parallel code, and opened with a dedicated session on parallelism and AI. Papers were selected through a single-blind review process, with three Program Committee reviews per submission, and appear in the order of the symposium program. Edited by Chong Li, Corinne Ancourt, and Gaetan Hains.

</details>

### [57] [Inference Pipelines as Operating-System Objects: Priority Scheduling and Constant-Footprint Streaming for Microcontroller Neural Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12614)
*Dimitrios Kafetzis*

Main category: cs.OS

TL;DR: 本文提出SynapticOS的Phase 2推理引擎，将MCU上的推理管道（预处理→加速器调用→后处理）作为一级OS对象管理，通过无堆的优先级作业调度器、精确尺寸的暂存区arena和流式恒定内存占用，在NXP FRDM-MCXN947上实现了215.8 FPS的人脸检测管道，调度开销仅92微秒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前MCU运行时将推理管道视为应用代码，每个项目都重复实现阶段排序、缓冲区大小控制和完成信号通知，缺乏操作系统层面的统一管理。

Method: 将推理管道设计为OS一级对象：从静态池中提取管道，验证阶段顺序，由优先级作业调度器（实时>正常>尽力而为，每类FIFO）执行，支持取消和有限作业表；推理路径无堆分配；阶段缓冲区根据配置和张量几何精确尺寸化，中间变量在逐帧重置的暂存区arena中管理。

Result: 在FRDM-MCXN947上调度开销92微秒；30帧六阶段人脸检测管道平均4.63ms/帧（215.8 FPS）；暂存区峰值2,784字节，每帧归零；QEMU软浮点下31.1ms。PowerQuad DSP加速5.51x（256点FFT）和1.66x（16x16矩阵乘法），未达10x目标。引擎占用3.8KB（QEMU）和20.7KB（FRDM）闪存，99测试全部通过。

Conclusion: 将推理管道作为OS一级对象管理在MCU上是可行的，流式恒定内存占用和精确缓冲区管理有效，但DSP加速效果未达预期目标，需进一步优化。

Abstract: Microcontroller runtimes treat the inference pipeline -- pre-processing, accelerator invocation, post-processing -- as application code: every project re-implements stage sequencing, buffer sizing, and completion signalling around a library call. We argue these are operating-system concerns and present the Phase 2 inference engine of SynapticOS, an open-source Zephyr-based runtime that makes the pipeline a first-class OS object. A pipeline is drawn from a static pool, validated against a canonical stage order, and executed by a priority job scheduler (realtime > normal > best-effort, FIFO per class) with cancellation and a bounded job table; no heap on the inference path. Stage buffers are sized exactly from configuration and tensor geometry for the nine built-in processors (bounded 4x fallback for user stages); all intermediates live in an ephemeral arena reset per frame, so streaming footprint is constant. We evaluate on the NXP FRDM-MCXN947 (Cortex-M33, 150 MHz) and the qemu_cortex_m3 CI target, both running a deterministic stub NPU kernel: engine-overhead baselines, not silicon throughput. On the board the scheduler adds 92 us over the Phase 1 direct-HAL bracket (1,130 vs 1,038 us; dispatch 1 us); a 30-frame, six-stage face-detection pipeline averages 4.63 ms/frame (215.8 FPS, stub model included) vs 31.1 ms under QEMU soft-float, at a constant 2,784-byte arena peak returning to zero each frame. The PowerQuad DSP is routed and self-calibrated for FFT and Q15 matmul; end-to-end speedups are 5.51x (256-point FFT) and 1.66x (16x16 matmul), short of the plan's 10x target -- reported as missed, not re-scoped. Stage-boundary profiling now runs live on the board, closing a Phase 1 gap. The engine adds 3.8 KB flash on QEMU and 20.7 KB on FRDM. 99 tests across 13 ZTEST suites pass 100% under emulation. Released under Apache 2.0 at https://github.com/Dimitrios-Kafetzis/SynapticOS

</details>


### [58] [Cross-Core Inference Offload as an Operating-System Service on Dual-Core Microcontrollers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12620)
*Dimitrios Kafetzis*

Main category: cs.OS

TL;DR: SynapticOS 是一个基于 Zephyr 的开源双核运行时系统，针对 NXP MCXN947 非对称双核 MCU（主核有 FPU/DSP/TrustZone/MPU，从核无）设计。AI 运行时驻留在功能完备的核心上，应用核心通过基于消息的远程系统调用访问推理服务。采用共享 SRAM 中的无锁 SPSC 环形缓冲区传输，无跨核原子操作，支持优先级、超时和零拷贝张量传输。实测：应用核心启动 1,514 μs，握手完成 2,554 μs，往返延迟典型 15 μs/最差 81 μs，推送仅 25 周期，1,913 次双模型浸泡测试零错误。固件占用 98.9 KB（运行时核）和 32.4 KB（应用核），108 个测试全部通过。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非对称双核 MCU（如 MCXN947，从核缺少 FPU、DSP、TrustZone、MPU）的异构特性未被充分利用，现有方案未将这种不对称性作为系统设计输入来优化 AI 推理任务的分配。

Method: 采用 Phase 3 双核架构：AI 运行时（模型、NPU/DSP、调度器）部署在功能完备的核心上，应用核心通过消息传递的 OS 服务（远程系统调用）发起推理请求。传输层使用共享 SRAM 中的无锁 SPSC 环形缓冲区，每个索引只有一个写入者，依赖 32 位自由运行计数器和数据内存屏障排序（平台无跨核原子操作）。支持优先级类、错误传播和超时机制，张量在共享槽中零拷贝传输（27 KB 帧不重复占用 64 KB RAM）。MPU 区域保护运行时核访问应用核 RAM（单向保护，已通过故障注入验证）。

Result: 在 FRDM-MCXN947（双核 150 MHz）上：应用核启动 1,514 μs，握手完成 2,554 μs，11 次启动位一致；往返延迟典型 15 μs/最差 81 μs（预算 50 μs）；推送操作 25 周期；1,913 次双模型浸泡测试零错误。固件占用：运行时核 98.9 KB 闪存，应用核 32.4 KB 闪存（RAM 42.6/64 KB）。108 个测试 13 个套件全部通过。报告了两个硬件暴露的缺陷：将第二个核释放到擦除的闪存会锁死整个芯片及其调试端口（已通过 ROM-API 空白检查预防）；Zephyr 闪存驱动 Kconfig 静默禁用了设备树 MPU 保护（已在运行时编程修复）。

Conclusion: 非对称双核 MCU 的异构特性可以被系统性地用作设计输入，通过消息传递架构和精心设计的无锁通信机制实现高效的 AI 推理分离。该方法在资源受限的嵌入式平台上验证有效，代码已开源（Apache 2.0）。

Abstract: Dual-core MCUs are asymmetric: on NXP's MCXN947, the second Cortex-M33 has no FPU, DSP extension, TrustZone, or MPU. We treat the asymmetry as a design input in the Phase 3 dual-core architecture of SynapticOS, an open-source Zephyr-based runtime: the AI runtime (models, NPU/DSP, scheduler) lives on the capable core, and the application core reaches inference only via a message-based OS service -- a remote system call. The transport is a pair of lock-free single-producer/single-consumer rings in shared SRAM: one writer per index, free-running 32-bit counters, ordering by data-memory barriers alone (the platform has no cross-core atomics). Because ring state is shared, a rebooting application core rejoins unaided. Requests carry priority classes, errors and timeouts propagate to the caller, and tensors stage zero-copy in a shared slot -- a 27 KB frame cannot exist twice in 64 KB of RAM. Measured on the FRDM-MCXN947 (both cores 150 MHz): the application core boots in 1,514 us and completes the handshake in 2,554 us, bit-identical over 11 boots; round trips are 15 us typical / 81 us worst-case (50 us budget); pushes cost 25 cycles; a 1,913-serve two-model soak had zero errors (stub-NPU latencies bracket transport, not silicon). An MPU region on the runtime core guards the application core's RAM (fault-injection verified); protection is one-directional -- the application core has no MPU, and ARMv8-M cannot block privileged reads. Two hardware-revealed defects are reported: releasing the second core into erased flash wedges the whole chip and its debug port (now prevented by a ROM-API blank check), and a Zephyr flash-driver Kconfig silently disarmed the devicetree MPU guard (now programmed at runtime). Firmware is 98.9 KB flash (runtime core) and 32.4 KB (application core, 42.6 of 64 KB RAM); 108 tests in 13 suites pass 100%. Apache 2.0: https://github.com/Dimitrios-Kafetzis/SynapticOS

</details>

### [59] [Optimal Adaptive Market Making: A Theoretical Framework for High-Yield Liquidity Provision in Perpetual Futures Markets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11888)
*Minmin Zeng,Yi Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个针对零做市商费用的永续合约市场的严格理论框架，将做市问题建模为随机最优控制问题，包含十个理论贡献（PnL分解、HJB方程、高APY制度定理、最优出入阈值、跨交易所对冲策略、鲁棒性边际、指数回撤概率界、遍历库存分布、凯利最优杠杆、多资产组合配置），并通过23张图的数值分析揭示了盈利与亏损制度间的相变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为现代去中心化永续合约交易所（零做市商费用）中的最优做市行为建立严格的理论基础，统一并扩展Avellaneda-Stoikov、Gueant-Lehalle-Fernandez-Tapia和Glosten-Milgrom等经典范式。

Method: 采用随机最优控制理论，在滤波概率空间上建模，控制变量为自适应买卖价差和跨交易所库存对冲决策。基于CARA效用函数推导HJB方程，并引入五个无量纲参数表征高APY制度，最终给出主APY公式。

Result: 得到了十个方面的理论结果，包括PnL分解定理、HJB方程与验证定理、高APY制度定理、最优出入阈值、对冲制度三分法、参数不确定性容忍度的鲁棒性边际、指数级回撤概率下界、遍历库存分布、凯利最优杠杆与破产边界、分散化饱和结果。数值实验通过23张图揭示了盈利与亏损间的相变。

Conclusion: 该框架为去中心化永续合约市场的做市提供了统一的理论基础，揭示了零费用环境下的独特微观结构特性，并为做市商提供了可操作的策略指导。

Abstract: We develop a rigorous theoretical framework for optimal market making in perpetual futures markets with zero maker fees. We model the market maker's problem as a stochastic optimal control problem on a filtered probability space, where the controls are adaptive bid-ask spreads and inventory hedging decisions across two exchanges. Our contributions include: (i) a PnL decomposition theorem separating revenue into spread income, adverse selection loss, inventory carrying cost, hedging friction, and funding rate exposure; (ii) the Hamilton-Jacobi-Bellman equation for the joint spread-inventory-hedging control problem under CARA utility with a verification theorem; (iii) High-APY Regime Theorems characterizing profitable regions via five dimensionless parameters, culminating in a Master APY Formula; (iv) analysis of zero-fee economics on decentralized perpetual exchanges with optimal entry-exit thresholds; (v) optimal cross-exchange hedging policies with funding rate dynamics and a hedge regime trichotomy; (vi) a robustness margin quantifying parameter uncertainty tolerance; (vii) exponential drawdown probability bounds and a universal APY-VaR identity; (viii) ergodic inventory distribution under optimal control with Bayesian adaptive estimation; (ix) Kelly-optimal leverage with ruin boundaries; and (x) multi-pair portfolio allocation with diversification saturation results. Numerical analysis with twenty-three figures reveals phase transitions between profitable and unprofitable regimes. Our framework unifies and extends the Avellaneda-Stoikov, Gueant-Lehalle-Fernandez-Tapia, and Glosten-Milgrom paradigms for modern decentralized venue microstructure.

</details>


### [60] [In-Context Reinforcement Learning under Non-Stationarity: A Survey](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11906)
*A Run,Ziluo Ding*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统综述了非平稳环境下的上下文强化学习（ICRL），聚焦于当部署策略参数固定时，智能体如何仅通过上下文推断变化的任务规则并适应环境。论文从“什么变化、如何变化、变化对智能体的可观测性”三个维度组织文献，识别了现有研究的不足并提出了未来方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ICRL综述主要围绕预训练目标、架构、上下文格式、评估协议和理论机制展开，非平稳环境（即环境动态变化）下的ICRL问题尚未得到充分关注。在变化环境中，累积的上下文可能过时、误导甚至产生干扰，亟需系统梳理该问题。

Method: 本文采用文献综述方法，将非平稳ICRL定义为“部署策略参数固定、仅通过上下文实现适应性”的问题，将其与元强化学习、决策序列建模、检索增强强化学习、值/模型感知ICRL及奖励反馈智能体进行关联，并从三个核心问题（什么变化、如何变化、变化可观测性）组织现有研究。

Result: 识别了非平稳ICRL中的关键挑战：奖励规范、转移核、观测通道、动作接口、约束模型、演示或记忆分布都可能发生变化；先前有用的上下文可能变得过时或误导，但也可能在旧环境回归时重新有用。文章建立了该领域的系统分类框架。

Conclusion: 非平稳ICRL是在不更新参数的前提下，让智能体通过上下文推断当前决策规则并判断哪些历史证据仍有效。该领域仍需更多研究来应对动态环境带来的独特挑战。

Abstract: The development of decision-pretrained transformers, algorithm distillation, long-context meta-RL, and retrieval-augmented agents has renewed interest in in-context reinforcement learning (ICRL): the ability of a pretrained or fine-tuned decision model to infer latent task rules and improve future behavior from interaction context, without test-time parameter updates. This line of work asks when trial-and-error evidence, rewards, transitions, demonstrations, feedback, or retrieved experience can make learning-like computation happen inside the context window. However, existing surveys of ICRL mainly organize the field around pretraining objectives, architectures, context formats, evaluation protocols, and theoretical mechanisms, while the non-stationary setting remains comparatively underexamined. In changing environments, accumulated context is not merely more evidence about a fixed task: the reward specification, transition kernel, observation channel, action interface, constraint model, or demonstration and memory distribution can fall out of alignment with the current regime. Previously useful context can therefore become stale, misleading, or useful again when an old regime returns. We survey non-stationary ICRL as the problem of adapting through context while deployed policy parameters remain fixed: the policy must infer both the current decision rule and which parts of its accumulated evidence still support that rule. We define non-stationary ICRL, relate it to meta-RL, decision sequence modeling, retrieval-augmented RL, value- and model-aware ICRL, and reward-feedback agents, and organize the literature along three questions: what changes, how the change unfolds, and how observable the change is to the agent.

</details>


### [61] [Ontology-Amplified Distillation and Contextuality Auditing for Sovereign Enterprise Language Models: A Combined Proof-of-Mechanism and Negative-Results Method Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11948)
*Thanh Luong Tuan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文结合两项研究：1）基于Qwen3.6-27B的本地蒸馏模型在越南金融领域任务上达到GPT-5水平的grounding率但统计效力不足；2）企业Agent路由的语境性审计方法显示零残留语境性。两项研究均不支持部署性、安全性、优越性、统计等价性或语境性正向路由规则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受数据驻留法规约束的金融机构需要能在机构内部运行的租户自有语言模型，同时需要有效的治理诊断方法来处理模型间的分歧。

Method: 1) 本体增强蒸馏：使用Qwen3.6-27B作为学生模型，通过前沿教师轨迹的监督微调和基于本体的DPO进行训练，在单台Apple M5 Max上使用47个合成偏好对。2) 语境性审计方法：对Phase 1.3各组进行Contextuality-by-Default分析。

Result: 蒸馏模型在40个越南金融任务中grounding 36个（grounded率0.90，本体覆盖率0.95），与GPT-5基线持平。但统计效力不足（95%CI跨±4任务），且未验证预设的超越假设。语境性审计显示零残留语境性。

Conclusion: 两项研究均不提供对部署性、安全性、优越性、统计等价性或语境性正向路由规则的支持性证据。

Abstract: Regulated financial institutions operating under data-residency rules need tenant-owned language models that can run inside the institution's perimeter. This paper combines two related FAOS studies into one mechanism-and-control article. First, it reports a reduced-power proof-of-mechanism study of ontology-amplified distillation: a Qwen3.6-27B student is adapted to the Foundation AgenticOS ontology through supervised fine-tuning on frontier-teacher trajectories and ontology-grounded direct preference optimization (DPO), trained locally on a single Apple M5 Max from 47 synthetic, English-language, cross-domain preference pairs. On 40 held-out Vietnamese financial-domain tasks, the distilled student grounds 36 of 40 tasks (grounded rate 0.90; mean ontology term-coverage r_onto = 0.95 on a metric floored at 0.50), equal to the GPT-5 frontier baseline, which also grounds 36 of 40. The outcome is underpowered to establish equivalence: the paired-difference 95% confidence interval spans +/-4 tasks, and the run does not test or show the pre-registered amplification prediction that the student should exceed the frontier. Second, the paper consolidates a contextuality-audit method for enterprise-agent routing. In a separate negative-results pilot, the corrected canonical Contextuality-by-Default degree is zero for all Phase 1.3 groups in both the local-Qwen run and an explicitly labeled Gemma replication check; the useful signal is direct influence and construct coupling, not surviving residual contextuality. Together, the studies pair an ontology-grounded model-building mechanism with a governance diagnostic for deciding when apparent disagreement should trigger prompt standardization, multi-agent synthesis, or human review. The evidence supports neither deployability, safety, superiority, statistical equivalence, nor a contextuality-positive routing rule.

</details>


### [62] [GRID: Grammar-Railed Decoding for Enterprise SQL Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11951)
*Mohsen Arjmandi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: GRID是一个基于LALR(1)解析器状态键控的语法约束解码引擎，提供可证明的SQL生成保障（完备性、终止性、近似常数时间代价），支持基于角色的访问控制编译入语法，通过哈希链审计追踪实现100%篡改检测，在Spider基准上以0.5B模型提升+13执行准确率，7B模型经修复后达94.5%可执行率，每token掩码中位数3.6-6.7微秒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业级SQL部署需要的不只是看似合理的文本，而是语法有效、遵循角色/模式策略、可证明（非尽力而为）的保障、生成规模增大不降速、以及合规级决策记录。现有方法无法同时满足这些要求。

Method: GRID使用LALR(1)解析器状态（词法扫描状态×LALR(1)栈）作为键计算精确的next-token掩码，以增量推进的解析器本身作为可行前缀预判器。LLM token通过字节级trie遍历桥接到语法终结符，采用上下文无关/上下文相关分割确保缓存键可靠性。基于角色的访问控制通过角色投影子集化语法产生式、模式词表限制标识符终结符编译入语言。使用Rust内核实现，每token掩码代价通过哈希链审计追踪记录。

Result: Rust内核每token掩码中位数3.6-6.7微秒，在两种tokenizer上p50和p90优于llguidance，零误拒。每token守卫代价在n=16,000时位置平坦。Spider上约束解码在0.5B模型提升+13执行准确率；一次检查器引导的修复pass将7B模型提升至94.5%可执行率。哈希链审计追踪100%篡改检测。

Conclusion: GRID提供了可证明的语法约束解码保障，在性能和准确率上均取得显著提升。但同时明确指出掩码无法做到的方面：分布忠实性、列级RBAC、非LALR(1)语言，以及剩余测量代价。

Abstract: Large language models can write SQL, but enterprise deployment demands more than plausible text: outputs must be syntactically valid, must respect per-role and per-schema policy, must carry provable (not best-effort) guarantees, must not slow down as generations grow, and must leave a compliance-grade record of every decision. We present GRID (Grammar-Railed Decoding), a grammar-constrained decoding engine that keys exact next-token masks on parser configurations (lexer scan state x LALR(1) stack) rather than on token sequences, and uses the incrementally advanced LALR(1) parser itself as a viable-prefix oracle. LLM tokens are bridged to grammar terminals by a byte-level trie walk with a context-independent/context-dependent split that makes cache-key soundness hold by construction. Role-based access control is compiled into the language: role projections subset the grammar's productions and schema lexicons restrict identifier terminals, so forbidden verbs and identifiers are unreachable at mask level. Four guarantees (soundness, completeness, termination, and near-constant per-token cost) are stated with explicit preconditions and each paired with a test or benchmark. Rust kernels bring the per-token mask to a 3.6-6.7 us median, ahead of llguidance at p50 and p90 on two tokenizers with zero false rejects; per-token guard cost is position-flat at n=16,000. On Spider, constrained decoding is worth +13 execution-accuracy points at 0.5B, and one checker-guided repair pass over the provably mask-unenforceable residue (column-level policy) lifts a 7B model to 94.5% executable. A hash-chained per-token audit trail replays bit-identically with 100% tamper detection. We state plainly what the mask cannot do (distribution faithfulness, column-level RBAC, non-LALR(1) languages) and where measured cost remains.

</details>


### [63] [Calibration-First Reward-Component Auditing for Reinforcement Learning Control in Smart Greenhouses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11959)
*Yuhui Bie,Guowei Xu,Yaojun Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出GreenLight-Gym框架，通过可复现的校准优先奖励审计方法，将温室强化学习中的标量奖励分解为温度、CO2、湿度等条件组件，并支持在模拟器训练、设施适应、竞赛记录和执行规则蒸馏等不同场景下进行组件对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单一的模拟器返回信号不足以指导智能温室控制， growers和工程师需要知道策略具体何时执行加热、CO2富集、通风、湿度管理、遮阳帘或补光等操作。

Method: 提出校准优先的奖励审计框架，将标量奖励分解为温度、CO2、湿度/蒸气压差、遮阳帘和执行代理等条件组件；将GreenLight模拟器适配到第二届自主温室挑战赛的气候记录；并在记录数据上评分相同组件。

Result: GreenLight-Gym框架使得温室控制奖励组件在模拟器训练、设施适应、竞赛记录和执行规则蒸馏等不同场景下保持可比性。

Conclusion: 该框架为智能温室控制提供了一种可复现、可分解的奖励审计方法，有助于理解策略的具体行为，而不仅仅是总回报。

Abstract: Greenhouse reinforcement learning can test climate-control ideas at a speed and scale that is difficult to achieve with crop experiments alone. For smart-greenhouse control, however, a single simulator return is not enough: a grower or control engineer also needs to know when the policy heats, enriches CO2, vents, manages humidity, deploys screens, or uses lamps.We propose a reproducible calibration-first reward audit framework that keeps named greenhouse-control reward components comparable across simulator training, facility-adapted rollouts, logged Autonomous Greenhouse Challenge records, and actuator-rule distillation. In GreenLight-Gym, the framework decomposes the scalar reward into conditional temperature, CO2, humidity and vapor-pressure-deficit, screen, and actuation-proxy terms; adapts GreenLight to the second Autonomous Greenhouse Challenge logged climate traces; and scores the same components on logged greenhouse data.

</details>


### [64] [Optimization Is Not All You Need](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11977)
*Minh Hua,Rita Raley*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文将GPT-2到GPT-3/4的对齐过程解读为"优化文化"的最新表现——一种认为沿预定轴线的可测量改进足以穷尽价值问题的信念。通过追踪这一信念在预训练、解码、偏好调优、基准测试、接口等整个技术栈中的运作，并追溯其在"审计社会"中的谱系，论文指出优化程序可以衡量生成文本的不可概率性，但无法区分这种"不可概率"是错误还是创新。然而这种缺乏判断能力的程序在五年内已获得了设定合法语言协议的权威，将原本属于学院、语法、考试官的评判权让渡给损失函数、奖励模型、基准测试和系统提示——一个执行判断职务却无判断能力的装置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 质疑将对齐(lignment)单纯视为工程成就的主流叙事，揭示其背后的文化逻辑——"优化文化"——并批判这种逻辑对语言价值判断的侵蚀。

Method: 理论分析/批判性谱系学方法。通过追踪"优化文化"在技术栈各层级（预训练、解码、偏好调优、基准测试、接口）的实现方式，并将其放归"审计社会"的谱系中进行历史性考察。

Result: 揭示出优化程序存在根本局限：可以度量文本的统计不可概率性，但无法区分这种不可概率是"错误"还是"创新"（即无法进行真正的价值判断）。

Conclusion: 在不到五年内，缺乏真正判断能力的优化装置已经篡夺了设定"合法语言"（egitimate language）协议的权威，这是一种从传统语言规范机构（学院、学校、语法、考官）向技术系统（损失函数、奖励模型、基准测试、系统提示）的权力转移，而后者"执行判断的职务却没有判断的能力"。

Abstract: In 2019, OpenAI released two million GPT-2 outputs-ungrammatical, half broken-to aid the detection of machine-generated text. The alignment that produced their more fluent successors is usually regarded as an engineering achievement; we read it instead as the newest expression of optimization culture: the conviction, older than the technology, that measurable improvement along predefined axes exhausts the question of value. Tracing that conviction through the stack-pretraining, decoding, preference tuning, benchmarking, interface-and back through its genealogy in the audit society, we arrive at the limit: an optimization procedure can measure how improbable a piece of generated text is; it cannot tell whether that unlikelihood is error or invention. A procedure that cannot make that distinction has nonetheless, within half a decade, assumed the authority to set the protocols of legitimate language. Held for centuries by academies and schoolrooms, grammars and examiners, this authority has been given over to loss functions, reward models, benchmarks, and system prompts: an apparatus that executes the office of judgment with no capacity for judging.

</details>


### [65] [LP Mining with LP2Graph: A Use Case for Railway Rescheduling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11980)
*Jörn Maurischat,Nikola Bešinović,Michael Färber*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出LP Mining with LP2Graph方法，能从已发表论文中自动挖掘LP/MILP模型结构，生成可重复数据集和客观分类体系，并通过重新求解验证模型正确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 铁路重调度领域的MILP建模知识分散在数百篇论文中，符号不兼容，传统综述按主观词汇而非结构分类，且无法复现模型。

Method: LP2Graph将每个公式表示为规范语法下的类型化变量-方程图；从规范模型出发，对源文献进行解析、同源化、自底向上聚类（变量→约束与目标→整体模型结构），并按应用领域和求解方法聚类；通过规则种子自更新分类器标注分组。

Result: 通过每簇代表模型独立生成LaTeX并跨CBC、HiGHS、Gurobi重新求解，与原文最优值对比验证模型正确性，得到客观、可重复的变量、约束和模型类型分类体系。

Conclusion: 该方法为raiLPminer自动化铁路重调度模型开发管线提供了原则性的基础，实现了客观、可重复的模型分类。

Abstract: Like many optimization-driven domains, railway rescheduling relies on Mixed-Integer Linear Programming (MILP), yet the field's modeling knowledge is scattered across hundreds of papers in incompatible notations, and narrative surveys organize it subjectively: they classify models by vocabulary rather than by structure, and reproduce neither. We present LP Mining with LP2Graph, a method that mines the structure of published LP and MILP formulations into a reproducible dataset and an induced taxonomy. Its core, LP2Graph, represents each formulation admitted by its canonical grammar as a typed variable--equation graph derived from a single canonical model; once a source is extracted into that model, everything downstream is deterministic. Each source is parsed into this model, homologized, and clustered bottom-up (over variables, then constraints and the objective, then whole-model structure) and, separately, by application domain and solution approach; the resulting groups are labeled by a rule-seeded, self-updating classifier. We validate the representation rather than assume it: per-cluster representatives are regenerated as independent LaTeX and re-solved across CBC, HiGHS and Gurobi against the optimum reported in the source paper. The outcome is an objective, repeatable taxonomy of variables, constraints and model types: the principled foundation on which our raiLPminer line of automated railway-rescheduling model development builds.

</details>


### [66] [Designing Agent-Ready Websites for AI Web Agents: A Framework for Machine Readability, Actionability, and Decision Reliability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12056)
*Said Elnaffar,Farzad Rashidi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"Agent就绪网站"设计框架，通过增强机器可读性、语义清晰度、可操作性和决策可靠性信号，使电商网站更适配AI代理的自主交互。实验表明，相比传统网站，该框架将严格成功率从49.3%提升至89.3%，步骤数从9.31降至6.49，显著提升了AI浏览器代理的可靠性和效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI代理独立执行搜索、比较和购买等电商任务的趋势增长，现有网站设计主要针对人类用户，缺乏对代理中介交互的优化支持。传统的Web设计、SEO和生成引擎优化（GEO）指标无法全面评估网站对AI代理的适配能力，因此需要一种新的设计框架来提升电商平台对AI代理的可读性、可解释性、可验证性和可操作性。

Method: 提出一个三维度框架：代理可解释性、代理可执行性和代理决策可靠性，涵盖机器可读性、语义清晰度、代理可操作性和上下文决策可靠性信号等特征。通过控制实验对比人类导向基线网站与Agent就绪版原型，二者拥有相同的商品目录、定价、库存和购物流程。实验涉及5个任务、3种浏览器代理模型（GPT-4.1、Gemini-2.5 Flash、Grok-4 Fast）和300次运行，评估指标包括PASS/PARTIAL/FAIL结果、严格与功能成功率、错误模式、步骤数和Token消耗。

Result: Agent就绪网站在150次运行中实现了134次PASS（基线为74次），严格成功率89.3% vs 49.3%，在商品详情提取、比较和多约束选择任务中提升最大。PARTIAL结果从43降至3，平均步骤数从9.31降至6.49。

Conclusion: 增强的结构清晰度、行动线索、证据信号和时间有效性指示符能够显著提升AI浏览器代理的可靠性和效率，为Agent就绪网站设计提供了初步实证支持。

Abstract: Online shopping is increasingly shifting toward a model in which AI agents independently search for products, compare options, evaluate constraints, and carry out parts of the purchasing process for users. Website design must now support both human and agent-mediated interaction. This paper introduces the agent-ready website, a design framework for enhancing the readability, interpretability, verifiability, and actionability of e-commerce platforms for AI agents. Existing web design, SEO, and generative engine optimization (GEO) metrics do not fully assess a website's capacity for agent-mediated interaction. The proposed framework is structured around three dimensions agent interpretability, agent executability, and agent decision reliability supported by features such as machine readability, semantic clarity, agent actionability, and contextual decision-reliability signals. The framework is evaluated through a controlled experiment comparing a human-oriented baseline and an agent-ready version of an identical website prototype, with identical catalogs, pricing, stock, and shopping workflows. The evaluation involved five tasks, three browser-agent models (GPT-4.1, Gemini-2.5 Flash, and Grok-4 Fast), and 300 runs, measuring PASS,PARTIAL,FAIL outcomes, strict and functional success rates, error patterns, step counts, and token consumption. The agent-ready website achieved 134 PASS runs out of 150 versus 74 out of 150 for the baseline (strict success rates of 89.3% vs. 49.3%), with the largest gains in product detail extraction, comparison, and multi-constraint selection. It also reduced PARTIAL outcomes from 43 to 3 and lowered the average step count from 9.31 to 6.49. These results provide preliminary evidence that enhanced structural clarity, action cues, evidence signals, and temporal validity indicators can substantially improve the reliability and efficiency of AI browser agents.

</details>


### [67] [Graph Feedback Controls Consensus and Clique Formation in Open-Weight Language-Model Populations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12077)
*Samer Saab,Chaouki Abdallah*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究开源权重语言模型群体中的惯例形成，通过命名游戏协议发现：保留合作伙伴标签记忆是达成共识的必要但不充分条件；同质性阈值相似性路由会加剧碎片化，而桥接式路由在记忆可用时能修复碎片化；在跨模型群体中，阈值相似性从未达成共识，而桥接式路由在保持记忆时能恢复行为共识；历史记忆总体上推动同质模型群体从碎片化走向共识。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体语言模型系统越来越普遍地使用局部交互路由，但运行时交互图常被视为实现细节而未被深入研究。本文试图系统性地研究惯例形成（convention formation）在开源LM群体中的机制，特别是不同路由策略对共识达成的影响。

Method: 采用命名游戏协议（naming-game protocol），使用限制性首token得分测量提示条件得分状态分布，构建状态相似性图，分离样本标签一致性与隐式状态空间共识。在1.1B-32B参数的开源LM群体上进行控制干预实验，比较阈值相似性路由与桥接式路由在不同记忆条件下的效果。

Result: (1) 保留合作伙伴标签证据是共识的必要但不充分条件；(2) 阈值相似性路由删除跨基线暴露并放大碎片化，桥接式路由在记忆可用时修复碎片化；(3) 在混合四模型网格中，阈值相似性在189次运行中未产生最终行为或状态共识，而桥接式路由在14/18的保持记忆运行中恢复行为共识；(4) 在同质模型群体中，保留历史记忆普遍将碎片化动态推向共识，Qwen2.5-32B在18个保持历史的充分混合设置中均达到稳定共识，而阈值相似性在189个设置中均未达成两种形式的共识；(5) 对状态阈值、群体规模和词汇量的鲁棒性分析保持定性排序，早期窗口图能量特征提供有用诊断。

Conclusion: 局部路由策略对多智能体LM系统的惯例形成有决定性影响：阈值相似性路由导致系统无法达成共识，而桥接式路由结合记忆机制可以有效促进共识形成。图能量特征可作为系统收敛性的早期诊断工具。

Abstract: Multi-agent language-model systems increasingly route local interactions, yet the runtime interaction graph is often treated as an implementation detail. We study convention formation in open-weight LM populations spanning 1.1B-32B parameters with a naming-game protocol. Restricted first-token scores over tokenizer-safe labels let us measure prompt-conditioned score-state distributions, construct state-similarity graphs, and separate sampled-label agreement from latent state-space consensus. Across controlled interventions, in the main open-weight repair grids, retained partner-label evidence is necessary but not sufficient: homophilous threshold-similarity routing deletes cross-basin exposure and amplifies fragmentation, while bridge-seeking routing often repairs fragmentation when memory is available. In a three-seed mixed four-model grid, threshold-similarity produces no final behavioral or state consensus in 189 setting-seed runs, whereas state-component and label-disagreement bridges recover final behavioral consensus in 14/18 retained-memory runs. Across homogeneous model populations, retained history generally shifts fragmented dynamics toward consensus; the clearest case is Qwen2.5-32B, which reaches stable behavioral and final state consensus in all 18 retained-history well-mixed settings, while threshold-similarity reaches neither form of consensus in 189 settings. Robustness over state thresholds, population size, and vocabulary size preserves the qualitative ordering, and early-window graph-energy features provide useful within-grid diagnostics.

</details>


### [68] [Operationalising Multi-Dimensional Evaluation for Conversational Agents: A Scalable, Governed Pipeline with Selective Re-evaluation and Model Benchmarking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12085)
*Niranjan Kumar M,Balaji Nagarajan,Karthik Nair,Faysal Satter,Nithin Surendran*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出GenAI Evaluation框架，一种受治理、配置驱动的流水线，用于大规模评估零售对话系统。该框架通过标准化、分片、异步执行和模式约束的LLM评分，评估有用性、真实性、清晰度、语气一致性和翻译质量，并支持选择性重评估、模式锁定、版本化配置和记录级溯源。每日处理约5万条记录，已评估超200万次交互，在12980条人工标注数据上达到0.93的宏F1分数和89%的翻译人工可接受度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 零售对话代理的评估需要超越词汇重叠指标的方法，以评估意图对齐、事实性、有用性、清晰度、语气和整体响应质量。虽然LLM作为评判者的方法提供了可扩展的替代方案，但生产部署引入了治理、可复现性、成本、模式一致性、可追溯性和可靠性方面的挑战。

Method: 提出GenAI Evaluation框架，是一个受治理、配置驱动的流水线，包括：标准化处理、分片、异步执行、模式约束的LLM评分；评估维度涵盖有用性、真实性、清晰度、语气一致性和翻译特定维度；采用选择性重评估（仅处理不完整、格式错误或模式无效的记录）；支持模式锁定、版本化配置、验证日志和记录级溯源。

Result: 每日处理约5万条记录，已评估超200万次交互。使用4名训练有素的标注员对12980条分层随机人工标注记录进行验证。分类覆盖14个意图、156个子意图、18个主要领域和129个子领域。流水线达到0.93的宏F1分数，翻译维度达到89%的人工可接受度准确率。

Conclusion: 该框架有效解决了生产环境中LLM评估的治理、可复现性、成本、模式一致性、可追溯性和可靠性挑战，为零售对话系统的大规模自动化评估提供了可行的工程化方案。

Abstract: Evaluating retail conversational agents requires methods beyond lexical-overlap metrics to assess intent alignment, factuality, helpfulness, clarity, tone, and overall response quality. Although LLM-as-a-judge methods provide scalable alternatives to human evaluation, production deployment introduces challenges in governance, reproducibility, cost, schema consistency, traceability, and reliability. We present GenAI Evaluation, a governed, configuration-driven pipeline for large-scale evaluation of retail conversational systems. It processes production chatbot logs through normalization, sharding, asynchronous execution, and schema-constrained LLM scoring. The framework evaluates helpfulness, truthfulness, clarity, tone alignment, and translation-specific dimensions. Selective re-evaluation processes only incomplete, malformed, or schema-invalid records, while schema locking, versioned configurations, validation logs, and record-level provenance support auditability. The framework processes approximately 50,000 records daily and has evaluated more than two million interactions.
