When applied to online handwriting, which is inherently represented as a time series of pen trajectories, such perturbations often introduce high-frequency jitter and visibly unnatural stroke artifacts. In this work, we propose a novel adversarial attack framework for online handwriting recognition based on salience-guided temporal editing. Instead of adding noise, the proposed method generates adversarial examples by inserting and deleting points at time steps selected according to temporal salience, preserving the shape and smoothness of the original handwriting. Temporal salience is estimated using gradient-based activation mapping, which guides edits toward time steps that strongly support the original class prediction. We evaluate the proposed approach on the Unipen and CASIA-OLHWDB datasets under both white-box and one-shot black-box attack settings. Experimental results demonstrate that while conventional image-based attacks achieve strong white-box performance, they exhibit poor transferability across models. In contrast, the proposed temporal editing attack achieves stronger one-shot black-box transferability while preserving the visual structure of the handwriting. These results indicate that temporal editing is a relevant threat model for online handwriting recognition, particularly in one-shot black-box transfer settings.

</details>


### [172] [What Does Goodness Measure? A Likelihood-Ratio Account of Forward-Forward Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12501)
*Paolo Giannitrapani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从显式生成模型角度重新解释Forward-Forward算法：平方 goodness 是两个零均值尺度不同总体的似然比检验充分统计量，FF阈值是其决策边界；推广到各向异性总体得到马氏距离 goodness，重尾总体得到饱和统计量（斜率即后验精度/除性归一化）；层间归一化需移除长度但保留每坐标能量（解释了单位范数归一化下的深度坍塌），而白化 goodness 可消除成对目标中的尺度膨胀捷径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FF算法中平方 goodness 和层间归一化通常被视为启发式方法，缺乏理论依据。作者希望从统计推断（似然比检验）和生成模型角度为这些选择提供理论解释，揭示其内在的统计意义。

Method: 将FF的平方 goodness 重新解释为两个零均值但尺度不同的多元正态总体的似然比检验充分统计量；将FF阈值解释为该检验的决策边界。然后推广到各向异性总体（得到马氏距离 goodness）和重尾总体（得到饱和统计量，其斜率为后验精度，实现除性归一化）。分析层间归一化必须移除向量长度同时保留每坐标能量，解释单位范数归一化导致的深度坍塌；证明成对目标存在尺度膨胀捷径，而白化 goodness 可消除此问题。

Result: 平方 goodness 是特定似然比检验的充分统计量；各向异性→马氏距离；重尾→饱和统计量（除性归一化）；层间归一化需保留每坐标能量（解释深度坍塌）；白化消除尺度膨胀捷径。

Conclusion: FF算法的启发式设计可以从统计检验和生成模型角度得到严格的理论解释，为理解和改进局部学习算法提供了统计理论基础。

Abstract: The Forward-Forward (FF) algorithm trains each layer locally, so that a scalar goodness - the sum of squared activations - is high on real inputs and low on contrastive ones, with activations normalized between layers. Both choices are usually treated as heuristics. Under an explicit generative model they are not: the squared goodness is the sufficient statistic of a likelihood-ratio test between two zero-mean populations differing in scale, and the FF threshold is its boundary. It generalizes: anisotropic populations yield a Mahalanobis goodness, the plain square being its isotropic case; heavy-tailed populations yield a saturating statistic whose slope is a posterior precision - divisive normalization - with bounded evidence and an advantage only under aggregation. The same lens characterizes the inter-layer normalization: it must remove the length while preserving per-coordinate energy, explaining a depth collapse we observe under unit-norm normalization; and the pairwise objective admits a scale-inflation shortcut that a whitened goodness removes.

</details>


### [173] [Learning Mechanistic Reasoning for Chemical Reactions with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12771)
*Xingyu Dang,Haocheng Tang,Junmei Wang,Yanjun Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文构建了大规模反应机理推理数据集，并建立了FukuyamaBench基准来评估层次化机理推理能力。微调的Qwen3-30B-A3B在精确路径匹配上达到8.3%，超过专用FlowER模型（5.1%），表明机理感知训练能显著增强语言模型的化学推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有化学大语言模型主要关注粗粒度的命名反应进行产物预测和逆合成分析，常导致物理不一致性和幻觉；而专门用于机理推断的小规模生成模型在跨化学空间泛化能力受限。因此需要构建大规模推理数据集来增强LLMs的机理推理能力。

Method: 构建了一个大规模反应机理推理数据集，并基于Fukuyama高级有机反应机理书籍建立了FukuyamaBench基准。在Qwen3-30B-A3B模型上进行机理感知微调。

Result: 微调的Qwen3-30B-A3B在FukuyamaBench Set A上达到8.3%的精确路径匹配，超过专用FlowER模型（5.1%）。

Conclusion: 机理感知训练能够显著增强语言模型的化学推理能力，为提升LLMs在化学领域的物理一致性和推理准确性提供了有效途径。

Abstract: Reaction mechanisms consist of the step-by-step sequences of elementary reactions that explain chemical transformations. Learning the mechanism logic is therefore essential for enhancing the fundamental chemical intelligence of large language models (LLMs). The stepwise deduction of reaction mechanism aligns naturally with the reasoning paradigms of reasoning LLMs. However, current chemical LLMs primarily emphasize coarse-grained name reactions for product prediction and retrosynthesis, often leading to physical inconsistencies and hallucinations. In contrast, specialized small-scale generative models for mechanism inference typically suffer from restricted generalization capacity across diverse chemical spaces. To overcome these limitations, we built a novel, large-scale reasoning dataset of reaction mechanisms. Furthermore, we established the FukuyamaBench, a difficult benchmark derived from Fukuyama's Advanced Organic Reaction Mechanism book, to rigorously evaluate model performance on hierarchical mechanism reasoning. Our fine-tuned Qwen3-30B-A3B achieves 8.3% exact pathway match on FukuyamaBench Set~A, surpassing the specialized FlowER model (5.1%), demonstrating that mechanism-aware training substantially enhances chemical reasoning in language models.

</details>


### [174] [OOD-RL-Bench: A Benchmark Framework for Out-of-Distribution Detection in Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12523)
*Emil Mittag,Richard Dazeley,Peter Vamplew*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了OOD-RL-Bench框架，用于评估强化学习轨迹中异常检测器的性能，发现观测扰动和机制切换易检测，而观测延迟和动作条件动力学难以检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有OOD检测基准通常评估图像分类器或静态低维数据集，未能考虑RL轨迹中复杂的、动作依赖的时间结构，缺乏专门评估RL轨迹中OOD检测器的统一框架。

Method: 设计OOD-RL-Bench框架，通过共享接口和配置集成检测器和异常注入器，支持新的评分方法和扰动族评估；使用DQN策略在LunarLander-v3环境中评估，采用匹配时间AUROC、AUPRC、假阳性率、检测延迟和分段起始指标。

Result: 观测扰动和机制切换被多种方法高精度识别，而观测延迟和动作条件动力学即使在比较同时间步的后异常分数与清洁分数时仍然难以检测。

Conclusion: OOD-RL-Bench为RL轨迹异常检测提供了可扩展的评估框架，揭示了不同异常类型下的检测难度差异，为未来RL安全性和稳健性研究提供了基准。

Abstract: Reliable reinforcement learning (RL) agents must maintain operational integrity amidst sensor malfunctions, dynamic disturbances, and slow environmental shifts. The detection of out-of-distribution conditions is pivotal to determining when an agent's observations, transitions, or trajectory dynamics deviate from the assumptions underpinning its policy training. Current out-of-distribution (OOD) detection benchmarks typically evaluate image classifiers or static low-dimensional datasets, failing to account for the complex, action-dependent temporal structure inherent in RL trajectories. To address this gap, we present OOD-RL-Bench, a comprehensive and extensible framework designed to evaluate OOD detectors against categories of anomalies injected into RL trajectories. Detectors and anomaly injectors are integrated through shared interfaces and configuration, which allows new scoring methods and perturbation families to be evaluated without modification of the core benchmark loop. We evaluate the utility of the framework using a Deep Q-Network policy within the LunarLander-v3 environment. We assess the performance of each detector across a suite of anomaly types using matched-time AUROC, matched-time AUPRC, matched-time false-positive rate, detection delay, and segmented-onset metrics. Our analysis reveals significant performance variance across anomaly types: observation perturbations and regime switches are identified with high accuracy by several methods, while observation delay and action-conditioned dynamics remain difficult even when post-onset anomaly scores are compared against clean scores from the same timesteps. We make the framework, trained policy checkpoint, and complete results publicly available as a reproducible artefact.

</details>


### [175] [From Preimage Search To Source-Grounded Feature Inversion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12526)
*Kaixiang Shu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出源接地特征反演（Source-Grounded Feature Inversion）方法，通过修复反向传播中的伴随信号，利用温纳滤波器将均值种子VJP映射为上游状态估计，并补偿JVP前向一致性残差，实现了无需逐查询优化的特征反演，可适用于CNN和Transformer等多种架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统特征反演方法通过目标匹配（寻找输入使其重编码特征与目标特征一致）来可视化神经网络内部特征，但由于存在多个输入满足该约束，无法唯一确定与生成该特征的原始样本相关联的逆映射，导致反演结果缺乏对源样本的依赖。

Method: 在计算图的每条边界上，利用反向传播提供的正确反向依赖关系，但修复其伴随信号：首先通过一个闭式矩阵温纳滤波将均值种子VJP映射到上游状态估计，再通过第二个温纳滤波补偿JVP前向一致性残差，将修复后的状态沿相同DAG组合一次反向传递即完成。该方法使用一组经过校准的零截距映射族，无需针对新输入、深度、通道等逐查询优化。

Result: 匹配目标和源的控制实验验证了每个逆映射依赖于所选特征和被解释样本的局部算子，而非独立于目标的图像模板。预测条件特征图谱将这些可视化结果与内部特征的独立干预对齐。

Conclusion: 源接地特征反演打开了模型隐藏的特征层次结构，能够在单个层和通道级别进行检验，将网络从输入中提取的内容与塑造其决策的内部证据联系起来。

Abstract: Interpreting a neural network requires understanding what its internal features extract from a particular input. Feature inversion seeks to express a selected feature in the input domain, but canonical iterative methods search for an input whose re-encoded representation matches the target. Because many inputs can satisfy this constraint, target matching alone does not specify the inverse associated with the sample that generated the feature. We formulate source-grounded feature inversion by conditioning the inverse on the source-local network geometry at the target-generating input. At each boundary of the computational DAG, backpropagation provides the correct reverse dependencies but transports an adjoint signal rather than an upstream-state estimate. We locally repair this signal with a closed-form matrix Wiener map from a mean-seed VJP to the upstream state, followed by a second Wiener map for the JVP forward-consistency residual, and compose the repaired states through the same DAG in one finite reverse pass. One calibrated zero-intercept map family supports new inputs, depths, channels, and channel groups across diverse CNN and Transformer architectures, tensor components, and visual distributions without query-specific optimisation. Matched target and source controls verify that each inverse depends on the selected feature and the local operators of the sample being explained, rather than a target-independent image template. Prediction-conditioned feature atlases align these visualisations with independent interventions on the corresponding internal features. Together, source-grounded feature inversion opens the model's hidden feature hierarchy to inspection at the level of individual layers and channels, linking what the network extracts from an input to the internal evidence that shapes its decision.

</details>


### [176] [Accelerating Masked Diffusion Large Language Models: A Survey of Efficient Inference Techniques](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12829)
*Daehoon Gwak,Minhyung Lee,Junwoo Park,Jaegul Choo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对扩散大语言模型（dLLMs）的推理效率问题，提出了统一的延迟分解框架，从算法、架构与系统、推理时扩展三个维度系统梳理了加速技术，并提供了可复现基准测试的指南。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散大语言模型虽具有并行生成的理论优势，但实际部署中端到端延迟受到算法、架构与系统层面因素的复杂交织影响，现有基准测试难以进行严格比较，亟需统一的分解框架来厘清各因素对推理速度的实际影响。

Method: 提出一个统一的延迟分解框架，用于解耦dLLMs推理中的算法、架构与系统级因素；基于该框架，从算法创新、架构与系统优化、推理时扩展三个维度对现有加速技术进行分类分析。

Result: 系统梳理了dLLMs推理加速的三大类技术路线；揭示了端到端延迟来源于多个层面因素之间的权衡关系；提供了可复现基准测试的指导方针。

Conclusion: 并行生成潜力的充分发挥仍面临开放挑战，统一延迟分解框架有助于推动dLLMs推理效率的标准化评估与实际部署优化。

Abstract: Diffusion large language models (dLLMs) offer a theoretical advantage in parallel generation over standard autoregressive models. However, parallel generation alone does not guarantee practical speedups. Realizing this efficiency requires specialized inference mechanisms, such as diffusion-aware caching and reuse. Consequently, as inference efficiency becomes a prerequisite for practical deployment, recent research has actively explored acceleration techniques across algorithms, architectures, and systems. However, rigorous comparisons remain difficult, as end-to-end latency stems from intricate trade-offs between algorithmic, architectural, and system-level factors that are often conflated in existing benchmarks. In this survey, we introduce a unified latency decomposition framework for dLLMs to disentangle these factors and analyze their impact on inference speed in real deployments. Guided by this framework, we categorize acceleration techniques along three axes covering algorithmic innovations, architectural and system optimizations, and inference-time scaling. Finally, we provide guidelines for reproducible benchmarking and highlight open challenges for realizing the full potential of parallel generation.

</details>


### [177] [Lightweight Multi-Scale Anomaly Detection for Resource-Constrained Edge Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12599)
*Raheen Junaid Wani,Smruti R. Sarangi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种轻量级多尺度自编码器（LMSAE），利用离散小波变换提取多尺度特征并采用多尺度损失函数，在保持小于500KB模型体积的同时，在时间序列异常检测中达到竞争性性能，并在Jetson Nano上实现9倍推理延迟降低和2倍功耗降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度学习方法在时间序列异常检测中计算开销大，且因缺乏多尺度敏感性而难以捕捉细微异常；自编码器虽广泛使用且适合轻量级部署，但需要提升其对多尺度输入的捕获能力。

Method: 提出轻量级多尺度自编码器（LMSAE），核心组件包括：离散小波变换（DWT）用于多尺度特征提取，以及多尺度损失函数用于增强对细微或隐藏异常的敏感性。

Result: 在基准数据集上达到竞争性或更优的检测性能；模型体积小于500KB；在NVIDIA Jetson Nano上推理延迟降低9倍，功耗降低2倍。

Conclusion: LMSAE是一种紧凑且计算高效的时间序列异常检测方法，适合资源受限的边缘部署场景。

Abstract: Time-series anomaly detection is increasingly important in IoT systems, sensor networks, and edge monitoring applications, where models must operate under strict constraints on memory, latency, and power consumption. While recent deep-learning approaches have improved detection accuracy, many remain computationally expensive and often fail to capture subtle anomalies due to limited multi-scale sensitivity. Autoencoders are widely used for anomaly detection because they reconstruct normal patterns well, leading to elevated reconstruction errors for anomalous inputs. Their simplicity and efficiency also make them suitable lightweight backbones for handling multi-scale inputs. To address these challenges, we propose a Lightweight MultiScale AutoEncoder (LMSAE) network for univariate time-series anomaly detection, designed to be compact and computationally efficient. LMSAE leverages the Discrete Wavelet Transform (DWT) to extract multi-scale features and employs a multi-scale loss function to improve sensitivity to subtle or hidden anomalies. Experiments on benchmark datasets demonstrate competitive or superior detection performance despite using significantly fewer parameters and a model size of less than 500 KB. LMSAE also achieves low-latency, low-power inference on the NVIDIA Jetson Nano, with 9x reduction in inference latency and 2x reduction in power consumption, making it ideal for edge deployment.

</details>


### [178] [The Geometry of Memorization: Finite-Time Spectral Sensitivity as a Diagnostic for Flow Matching Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12616)
*Shuchan Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于有限时间谱灵敏度(FTSS)的梯度无关前向传播指标，通过追踪状态转移矩阵的均方根奇异值来揭示连续时间生成框架中的流形几何结构，并发现过拟合会导致谱崩塌这一几何病理现象，进而开发出无需外部查询即可检测生成记忆化的内部几何审计方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续时间生成框架在理论目标上偏好直线轨迹，但经验网络却发展出复杂的路径形变。现有方法难以直观理解这种几何行为，且检测生成记忆化需要外部成员查询或基线数据比较，缺乏基于内部动态的自监督检测手段。

Method: 提出FTSS g(t)指标，通过计算状态转移矩阵的均方根奇异值，作为稳定秩的连续代理，以无梯度方式在前向传播中揭示流形几何结构。利用该指标观察数据稀缺条件下的几何病理：泛化模型保持稳定有效维度，而过拟合模型出现谱崩塌。基于此结构现象构建内部几何审计框架。

Result: FTSS能够有效区分泛化模型与过拟合模型：泛化模型保持稳定的有效维度，而过拟合模型表现为谱崩塌。基于g(t)的内部几何审计方法能够仅依靠内部轨迹动力学检测生成记忆化，无需外部成员查询或基线数据比较。

Conclusion: 本文证明连续时间生成框架中的流形几何结构可作为过拟合和记忆化的内在指示器，所提出的FTSS指标为检测生成模型记忆化提供了一种无需外部数据对比的自监督方案。

Abstract: Continuous-time generative frameworks construct probability paths between base and target domains by optimizing time-dependent velocity fields. While theoretical targets favor straight trajectories, empirical networks develop complex path deformations. This paper presents the Finite-Time Spectral Sensitivity (FTSS) g(t), a gradient-free, forward-pass metric that exposes flow geometry by tracking the root-mean-square singular value of the state-transition matrix. Serving as a continuous proxy for stable rank, g(t) reveals a distinct geometric pathology under data scarcity: while generalizing models maintain stable effective dimensions, overfitting causes a spectral collapse. We leverage this structural phenomenon to develop an internal geometric audit based on g(t). Our framework detects generative memorization using purely internal trajectory dynamics, removing the need for external membership queries or baseline data comparison.

</details>


### [179] [Learning Forced Multibody Dynamics on Lie Groups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12627)
*Martine Dyring Hansen,Marta Ghirardelli,Elena Celledoni,David Martin de Diego,Brynjulf Owren*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于离散受迫欧拉-拉格朗日方程在Lie群上的架构，仅使用位置数据学习机械系统动力学，天然保持几何结构与守恒律，适用于多体系统和含噪声环境。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法通常依赖速度数据或无视系统的几何结构，而实际中速度数据可能不可用或含有噪声，因此需要一种仅基于位置观测且能保持系统几何不变性的动力学学习方法。

Method: 将机械系统动力学直接公式化在流形值位形空间上，利用离散受迫欧拉-拉格朗日方程在Lie群上构建学习架构，仅使用位置数据作为输入。

Result: 该方法在合成数据集和真实世界数据集上均表现优异，并能自然地扩展到多体系统和适应外部控制输入。

Conclusion: 所提出框架在不依赖速度数据的情况下，能有效学习机械系统的动力学，同时保持系统的几何结构和守恒律，具有广泛的应用前景。

Abstract: We propose an architecture for learning the dynamics of mechanical systems based on discrete forced Euler-Lagrange equations on Lie groups using only position data. By formulating the dynamics directly on manifold-valued configuration spaces, the method naturally respects the geometric structure of the systems and preserves geometric invariants and conservation laws. The reliance on position measurements alone makes the framework applicable in settings where velocity data are unavailable or noisy. The approach extends naturally to multibody systems, accommodates external control inputs, and demonstrates strong performance on both synthetic and real-world datasets.

</details>


### [180] [AdaPCLA: Adaptive Prior-Calibrated Logit Adjustment for Long-Tailed Longitudinal EHR Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12645)
*Shuai Cui,Chen Wenxuan,Wenjie Du,Jian Lou,Dan Li,Wenjie Feng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AdaPCLA提出了一种数据分布感知的训练策略，通过模拟退火训练内化数据知识参数，并支持零样本分布控制实现无训练适应不同临床人群，在保留罕见事件共现结构和提升生成数据保真度方面取得了显著效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准自回归模型在生成纵向电子健康记录数据时，倾向于低估罕见事件（如疾病、症状）的共现结构，导致对罕见亚群生成数据的保真度和忠实度降低，影响了隐私保护研究的可靠性。

Method: 提出AdaPCLA框架：1) 数据分布感知的训练策略，通过模拟退火训练内化数据知识参数；2) 支持零样本分布控制，实现无训练适应不同临床人群的生成；3) 通过标签经验神经正切核（NTK）理论分析罕见代码的logit更新，推导退火速度与NTK条件数对先验信号保留的约束边界。

Result: 在真实数据上，AdaPCLA在罕见对共现（TailPairSeen）上相比HALO在MIMIC-III提升114.2%、MIMIC-IV提升65.1%；零样本跨人群适应的F1分数比GPT风格生成提升3.5%。

Conclusion: AdaPCLA通过分布感知的训练策略和零样本控制，有效提升了生成EHR数据中罕见事件的保真度，在罕见对共现、下游任务效用和零样本控制方面均取得一致性增益。

Abstract: Generative modeling of longitudinal Electronic Health Records is increasingly important for privacy-preserving research, yet standard autoregressive models tend to underrepresent the co-occurrence structure of tail events (i.e., diseases, symptoms), reducing the fidelity and faithfulness of generated data for rare subpopulations. To this end, we propose AdaPCLA framework, which enables generative models to adaptively fit and generate EHR data through a data distribution-aware training strategy; this is achieved by internalizing data knowledge parameters by simulated annealing training. It also supports training-free adaptation to a diverse clinical population for generation through zero-shot distribution control. Moreover, our theoretical analysis characterizes rare-code logit updates through the label-wise empirical NTK and derives a prior-internalization bound for how annealing speed and NTK conditioning affect retained prior signals. Experiments on real-world data show that AdaPCLA achieves consistent gains in tail plausibility, downstream utility, and zero-shot control; in particular, it improves TailPairSeen over HALO by 114.2% on MIMIC-III and 65.1% on MIMIC-IV, outperforms GPT-style generation by 3.5% F1 for zero-shot cross-population adaptation.

</details>


### [181] [Evidence-Grounded Verified Agentic Reasoning: A Path Toward Eliminating LLM Hallucination in Empirical Inference via Tool-Attested Kernel Proofs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12650)
*Junyu Ren*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出EG-VAR，一种基于Lean 4的架构，通过内核验证确保LLM工具调用输出的每一条声明都源自被证实的工具调用和可验证的推理链，无法验证的输出则明确声明为"Abstain"并附带可重放的审计轨迹。在TableBench数值推理子集上达到120/120，在反事实压力测试中保持100%源忠实度，同时将语义形式化错误控制在1.7%-3.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM即使使用工具，其经验推理输出仍无法保证可治理性：接受输出不一定源于被证实的证据，接受推理也不一定经得起形式化检验。需要一种机制确保输出在结构上可追溯至被证实的工具调用和内核验证的推理链。

Method: 基于Lean 4的形式化验证架构：Lean内核作为唯一"Verified"声明的铸造者，通过工具证明公理和声明源提升机制，确保每个已验证输出在结构上源自被证实的工具调用（定理3.1）和内核检查的有效推理链（定理3.2），否则输出"Abstain"及可重放审计轨迹。

Result: TableBench数值推理子集（n=120）上达到120/120，对比基线95%；反事实压力测试（5领域×2模型）中EG-VAR保持100%源忠实度，相同工具基线降至80-90%，无工具基线降至50-80%。语义形式化错误率：Sonnet 3.3%，Opus 1.7%。

Conclusion: EG-VAR可作为高风险经验声明的技术治理接口：形式化侧车使目标命题、源范围、证据边界、证明义务和弃权条件均可审计，消除无支撑的"Verified"输出，并将形式化错误、源权威争议、歧义和弃权转化为显式审计目标。长远看，数据集、API、公共记录和AI生成文档中的类型化侧车可将形式化负担摊薄为可复用基础设施。

Abstract: Tool access alone does not make LLM empirical reasoning governable: accepted outputs need not descend from attested evidence, and accepted deductions need not hold up under formal scrutiny. We present EG-VAR (Evidence-Grounded Verified Agentic Reasoning), a Lean 4-based tool-calling architecture in which the Lean kernel is the sole minter of Verified claims via tool-attestation axioms and declared source lifts. Every verified output structurally descends from an attested tool call (Thm. 3.1) and a kernel-checked chain of valid inference (Thm. 3.2); residual outputs are honest Abstain with a replayable audit trail. On a subcollection of TableBench numerical reasoning (n=120), EG-VAR attains 120/120 versus a 95% same-tool baseline; on counterfactual stress tests (5 domains x 2 models), EG-VAR stays 100% source-faithful while same-tool drops to 80-90% (no-tool 50-80%). With the LLM as deployment-time formalizer, residual semantic-formalization error is 3.3% on Sonnet and 1.7% on Opus. We position EG-VAR as a technical-governance interface for high-stakes empirical claims: a formal sidecar makes the target proposition, source scope, evidence boundary, proof obligation, and abstention condition auditable, eliminating unsupported Verified outputs today while turning formalization errors, lift and source-authority disputes, ambiguities, and abstentions into explicit audit targets. Over time, typed sidecars in datasets, APIs, public records, and AI-generated documents can amortize this formalization burden into reusable infrastructure.

</details>


### [182] [Learning-based Probabilistic Load Forecasting with Post-hoc and In-model Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12730)
*Sarah Al-Shareeda,Gulcihan Ozdemir,Heung Seok Jeon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了智能建筑负荷预测中，当部署时输入特征缺失需重构时，不确定性应在模型何处放置的问题。通过对比模块化事后残差分位数方案与集成式模型内分位数学习方案，在三种深度学习骨干网络（循环、混合循环、注意力机制TFT）下实验，发现不确定性放置位置依赖于骨干网络，TFT+集成分位数学习效果最佳，且重构输入会显著增加分位数评分（106%）而区间宽度几乎不变，表明模型无法自动吸收重构带来的不确定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能建筑负荷预测模型通常基于密集、多变量、高频数据离线训练，但实际部署时可能只有低分辨率、少特征输入。缺失特征需要重构，而重构误差会通过模型传播。若输入不确定性不被反映，预测区间会失准，影响需求响应调度。现有研究对于输入重构后的不确定性应在模型何处放置缺乏系统分析。

Method: 开发统一的日前概率预测框架，对齐时间分辨率、重构缺失输入、导出因果特征。对比两种方案：模块化事后残差-分位数方案（post-hoc residual-quantile）与集成式模型内分位数学习方案（in-model quantile-learning）。使用三种深度学习骨干：循环模型、混合循环模型、基于注意力的Temporal Fusion Transformer（TFT）。在相同输入、预测范围、预处理规则和训练预算下比较。使用Diebold-Mariano检验进行统计验证，并进行了重构敏感性测试和鲁棒性检验。

Result: 不确定性放置位置依赖于骨干网络。集成分位数学习在TFT上最可靠，标记测试窗口上MAPE为2.2-3.6%，RMSE为28-83W，区间宽度比模块化方案在最近似名义覆盖率下窄约5倍。Diebold-Mariano检验支持TFT排序和循环骨干的混合表现。重构敏感性测试显示重构输入使分位数评分（QS）增加106%，而区间宽度几乎不变。鲁棒性检验支持排名。

Conclusion: 事后残差分位数方案在推理依赖重构输入时存在局限性。模型无法自动吸收重构带来的不确定性，需要针对性设计不确定性建模方法。TFT配合集成分位数学习是处理缺失特征重构场景下概率负荷预测的最优方案。

Abstract: Smart-building load forecasters are often trained offline on dense, multivariate, high-frequency data, but deployment may provide only hourly, feature-limited inputs. Missing features must then be reconstructed, and their errors can propagate through the model. If this input uncertainty is not reflected, prediction intervals may become miscalibrated, affecting demand-response scheduling. Our work examines where uncertainty should be placed once inference inputs are reconstructed. We develop a unified one-day-ahead probabilistic forecasting framework that aligns temporal resolution, reconstructs the unavailable inputs, and derives causal features, and we compare a modular post-hoc residual-quantile scheme with an integrated in-model quantile-learning scheme. The comparison uses three mid-scale Deep Learning (DL) backbones: recurrent, hybrid recurrent, and attention-based Temporal Fusion Transformer (TFT) models, under identical inputs, forecasting horizon, preprocessing rules, and training budgets. Results show that uncertainty placement is backbone-dependent. Integrated quantile learning is most reliable with the TFT, yielding 2.2-3.6% MAPE and 28-83W RMSE on the labeled test window, while producing intervals about 5x narrower than the modular intervals at the closest-to-nominal coverage level. Diebold-Mariano tests support the TFT ranking and the mixed behavior of the recurrent backbones. A reconstruction-sensitivity test shows that reconstructed inputs increase the Quantile Score (QS) by 106% while interval width remains nearly unchanged, indicating that the model does not automatically absorb reconstruction-induced uncertainty. Robustness checks against non-DL baselines and seasonal hold-out weeks support this ranking. Our results expose the limits of post-hoc residual quantiles when inference depends on reconstructed inputs.

</details>


### [183] [What Makes a Representational Prior Work? Feature Families, Label-Free Invariances, and Critical Windows in Grokking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12735)
*Gunner Levi Howe*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过188组实验，系统研究了对比先验在grokking现象中的作用机制，发现先验在特征族层面运作，无标签的不变性先验即可加速泛化，且仅在训练早期需要，能将权重范数延迟律指数降低约17倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解grokking延迟的因果机制，验证并扩展伴随工作中关于对比先验可注入任务结构化表征的发现，探究先验生效的条件和边界。

Method: 沿四个维度（内容、监督信号、时序、设置）进行188组控制实验，系统比较不同先验设计（错误特征族、无标签不变性、不同时间窗口）在模块化乘法等任务上的表现。

Result: 错误特征族先验与随机划分无显著差异；无标签不变性先验在15/15运行中泛化，中位加速2.7倍，优于有监督先验；仅前2000轮应用先验即可在10/10运行中实现2.7倍加速；先验使权重范数延迟律指数降低约17倍。

Conclusion: 先验的有效性取决于特征族对齐，不变性内容足以在无标签情况下加速泛化，早期窗口捕获了绝大部分收益，且先验能大幅平缓权重范数的延迟律。

Abstract: Companion work showed the grokking delay is causally the time to form task-structured representations, injectable via a contrastive prior. Here we characterize what makes such a prior work, across four axes, in 188 new runs. Content: a coherent, learnable prior built from the wrong feature family (magnitude bands) blocks generalization like a random partition (1/15 vs 0/20 grok; $p=0.43$ between them), confirming the companion's prediction that priors act at the level of the circuit's features. Supervision: a fully label-free invariance prior -- positives are commuted pairs $(a,b)\sim(b,a)$ only -- generalizes in 15/15 runs at a median $2.7\times$ speedup, more reliably than the label-supervised prior itself ($p=0.038$), and combined with a weight-norm clamp yields the strongest method we test (median $17\times$, 5/5) -- strongest meaning reliably fast: plain cross-entropy with a clamp matches this speed only at the exact critical norm, while the prior keeps it fast across the entire clamp range. Timing: the prior is only needed early -- applied solely during the first 2000 epochs (4% of budget) it generalizes 10/10 at $2.7\times$, beating continuous application (8/10, $1.25\times$) and a duration-matched later window ($2.1\times$). Setting: the dissociation replicates on modular multiplication and across depths and normalization variants, and a clamp sweep quantifies the companion's central claim: structure injection flattens the weight-norm delay-law exponent about 17-fold (plain cross-entropy slows $31\times$ per +10 norm units, a lower bound as higher cells are censored, versus $1.22\times$ with the prior). Honest boundary: tasks that generalize before memorizing have no delay to control. Feature-family alignment decides whether a prior permits generalization; invariance content suffices for acceleration without labels; a brief early window captures nearly all of the benefit.

</details>


### [184] [Constraint-Aware Aggregation for Federated Reinforcement Learning in Microgrid Energy Coordination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12763)
*Usman Haider,Karl Mason*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于惩罚机制的约束感知聚合方法（权重与奖励减去违规成正比），用于联邦强化学习中的分布式能源协调，在无需修改本地训练或对偶优化的前提下，显著降低了违规行为并提升了奖励，优于标准FedAvg方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准FedRL聚合方法（如FedAvg）未考虑系统级约束，导致全局行为不安全；需要一种能够兼顾奖励与安全性的轻量级聚合策略。

Method: 提出惩罚基聚合规则，服务器端权重更新为 $w_i \propto R_i - αV_i$（奖励减去估计违规乘以系数），无需对偶优化或修改本地训练；此外还探索了奖励-违规联合方案。

Result: 在DairyGridEnv基准（多农场电池储能协调）及芬兰/德国FIELD真实负载数据上，惩罚基聚合相比FedAvg显著减少违规并提高奖励；奖励-违规联合方案虽提供可调权衡但稳定性较差。

Conclusion: 轻量级聚合策略能在保持标准通信协议的同时，实质性地提升联邦强化学习的经验安全性。

Abstract: Federated Reinforcement Learning (FedRL) enables coordination of distributed energy resources without sharing raw local data, but standard aggregation methods such as FedAvg do not account for system-level constraints, often leading to unsafe global behavior. In this work, we study constraint-aware aggregation for federated reinforcement learning in distributed energy coordination. We propose aggregation rules that incorporate both local performance and estimated constraint violation into the server-side update. Among these, a simple penalty-based rule, $w_i \propto R_i - αV_i$, consistently provides the most reliable trade-off between reward and safety, without requiring dual optimization or modifications to local training. \textcolor{black}{We evaluate our approach on DairyGridEnv, a benchmark modeling multiple farms coordinating battery storage under stochastic demand and a shared grid capacity constraint, and further assess robustness using real load-driven demand profiles from Finland and the German FIELD dataset. Across multiple seeds, penalty-based aggregation substantially reduces violations while improving reward relative to FedAvg in both synthetic and real load-driven settings.} A combined reward-violation scheme exposes a tunable trade-off via $λ$, but is less stable. These results demonstrate that lightweight aggregation strategies can substantially improve empirical safety in federated reinforcement learning while preserving standard communication protocols.

</details>


### [185] [AVQ-Attention: Adaptive Vector-Quantized Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12789)
*Winfried van den dool,Patrick Forré,Amir Habibian,Yuki M. Asano,Max Welling*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出自适应向量量化注意力（AVQ-Attention），通过自适应分配码本容量来优化注意力机制，在保持O(MN)复杂度下实现比固定码本VQ注意力更好的精度-效率权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准注意力O(N²)复杂度是Transformer的计算瓶颈；VQ注意力虽降至O(MN)，但均匀分配码本容量导致高注意力区域近似粗糙、低注意力区域浪费容量。

Method: 从少量码字出发，在前向传播中识别重要码字，用预学习的子码字对其进行细化，实现重要区域细粒度量化、其他区域粗粒度量化；采用自定义Triton内核在Flash Attention的平铺计算范式内完成自适应细化全过程。

Result: 保持了O(MN)复杂度，相比固定码本VQ注意力实现了更优的精度-效率权衡。

Conclusion: AVQ注意力通过自适应码本分配有效解决了VQ注意力中容量利用不均的问题，为Transformer注意力计算提供了一种更高效的近似方案。

Abstract: The $\mathcal{O}(N^2)$ complexity of attention over $N$ tokens remains a computational bottleneck in transformer models. Vector-Quantized (VQ) attention reduces this to $\mathcal{O}(MN)$ by representing keys with $M$ codewords, but applies uniform codebook capacity regardless of where attention mass concentrates: high-attention regions of key space may be coarsely approximated while low-attention regions waste representational capacity. We propose Adaptive Vector-Quantized (AVQ) Attention, which adaptively allocates codebook capacity based on attention importance. Starting from a small set of codewords, our method identifies the most important codes during the forward pass and refines them with pre-learned child codewords, achieving fine-grained quantization where it matters most while maintaining coarse quantization elsewhere. We develop an implementation using custom Triton kernels that enables the full adaptive refinement process, including importance scoring, child codeword insertion, and parent contribution replacement, to be carried out within the tiled computation paradigm of Flash Attention with minimal overhead. Our approach maintains $\mathcal{O}(MN)$ complexity while achieving improved accuracy-efficiency trade-offs compared to fixed-codebook VQ-attention.

</details>


### [186] [Verifier-Based Reinforcement Fine-Tuning of Reasoning Models for Thermal Energy Storage Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12856)
*Takumi Shioda,Kohei Terashima,Tatsuo Nagai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过强化学习与可验证奖励（RLVR）微调开源推理模型，使其在建筑热储能调度任务中接近动态规划最优解，排放仅61.2 kg-CO₂（最优60.8），并发现推理时间扩展是关键。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建筑需要响应电网条件转移冷负荷，但热储能（TES）调度需要提前数小时规划，现有MPC和强化学习难以规模化推广，急需一种可扩展的高效调度方法。

Method: 将精确离线动态规划（DP）的动作值转换为稠密奖励，仅用30条训练提示对开源推理模型进行强化微调（RFT），使其作为上层调度器输出每小时热泵设定点，输入为基于文本的状态和预测信息。

Result: RFT将排放从70.5降至61.2 kg-CO₂，接近DP最优的60.8；GPT-5无需训练即接近DP和MPC，GPT-4o（非推理LLM）排放高于无储能基线；RFT主要稳定了候选比较、前瞻和可行性检查等规划模式，且这些模式在预测误差和未见TES条件下保持，可迁移至电池任务。

Conclusion: 基于DP的可验证奖励为适配开源推理模型到建筑储能调度提供了实用途径，激励更高保真度的全建筑控制和城市级能源管理的可扩展验证器研究。

Abstract: Buildings are expected to shift cooling loads in response to grid conditions. Thermal energy storage (TES) enables this shift, but scheduling it well requires planning hours ahead under storage constraints. Model predictive control (MPC) and reinforcement learning are difficult to scale across buildings. This study instead adapts an open-weight reasoning model through reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR). We convert exact offline dynamic-programming (DP) action values into dense rewards for every candidate action. Using only 30 training prompts, reinforcement fine-tuning (RFT) trains the model as an upper-level scheduler that outputs hourly heat-pump setpoints from text-based states and forecasts. Evaluation uses a deliberately simple office-building TES benchmark where exact DP is tractable and the optimum is known. RFT reduces the open-weight model's emissions from 70.5 to 61.2 kg-CO2, close to the DP optimum of 60.8 kg-CO2. GPT-5 nearly matches DP and MPC without task-specific training, while GPT-4o, a non-reasoning LLM, produces higher emissions than the no-storage baseline, so inference-time reasoning appears important. Trace analysis shows that RFT mainly stabilizes observable planning patterns (candidate comparison, look-ahead, and feasibility checking) rather than creating a new strategy. Robustness and generalization tests clarify what transfers: the reinforced planning patterns persist under forecast errors and an unseen TES condition and carry over to a battery task, but its different structure limits the gains. DP-based verifiable rewards offer a practical way to adapt open-weight reasoning models to building storage scheduling. These results motivate higher-fidelity tests of whole-building control and scalable verifiers for city-scale energy management.

</details>


### [187] [Energy-Based Physics-Informed Form Finding for Clustered Tensegrity Structures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12888)
*Jing Qin,Muhao Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于能量的学习框架，用于簇式张拉整体结构找形与物理属性预测，通过将总势能最小化和本构关系融入训练目标，同时预测平衡节点构型、杆件内力及力密度，在棱柱和张拉整体着陆器系统上验证了其可扩展性与准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 张拉整体找形与物理属性预测是结构力学中的基本逆问题，由于几何与力耦合导致的强非线性、结构稳定性保障以及边界条件、对称性等约束的强制执行，传统方法往往缺乏对噪声和异常值的鲁棒性，亟需一种更鲁棒、物理一致性更强的求解框架。

Method: 提出基于能量的学习框架，将总势能最小化和本构关系作为物理损失项纳入训练目标，实现同时预测平衡节点构型、杆件内力和力密度，通过能量驱动的物理损失提升物理一致性、鲁棒性和数据效率。

Result: 在棱柱和张拉整体着陆器系统上的数值实验表明，该方法在可扩展的找形和结构属性精确预测方面具有巨大潜力，验证了框架的有效性。

Conclusion: 基于能量的学习框架有效解决了张拉整体结构找形与物理属性预测中的非线性与鲁棒性问题，实现了物理一致、数据高效且可扩展的求解方案。

Abstract: Tensegrity form-finding and physical property prediction are fundamental inverse problems in structural mechanics, which aim to determine equilibrium configurations and internal force distributions. These problems are challenging due to strong nonlinearity arising from the coupling between geometry and forces, the need to ensure structural stability, and the enforcement of constraints such as boundary conditions and symmetry. Moreover, traditional methods often lack robustness to noise and outliers. This paper proposes an energy-based learning framework for clustered tensegrity form finding and physical property prediction. The proposed approach incorporates total potential energy minimization and constitutive relations into the training objective, enabling the simultaneous prediction of equilibrium nodal configurations and associated physical quantities, including member forces and force densities. By incorporating energy-based physical losses directly into the learning process, the framework improves physical consistency, robustness, and data efficiency. Numerical experiments on tensegrity structures, including prism and lander systems, show the great potential of the proposed approach and demonstrate its capability for scalable form finding and accurate prediction of structural properties.

</details>


### [188] [Contrastive-Collapsed Loss for Flexible and Geometrically Optimal Embeddings and Faster Convergence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12916)
*Blanca Cano-Camarero,Ángela Fernández-Pascual,José R. Dorronsoro*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CoCo损失函数，通过鼓励类内坍塌和类间对比来学习归一化且结构良好的表示，在OpenML-CC18表格数据集上取得了与SOTA方法（核SVM、随机森林、点回归和交叉熵神经网络）相当的性能，并具有更紧致的类聚类和更快收敛的优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有损失函数（如交叉熵和点回归）在学习归一化且结构良好的表示时存在初始化距离最优配置较远、梯度信息不足、类表示坍塌不够充分等问题，需要一种能同时兼顾类内紧凑性和类间可分离性的目标函数。

Method: 提出CoCo损失函数，结合类内坍塌（intra-class collapse）和类间对比（inter-class contrast）机制，允许神经网络逼近具有大角度类间间隔的几何最优嵌入。从理论上分析了CoCo与点回归、交叉熵的关系，证明其更接近最优初始化、提供更有信息量的梯度以及更强的类坍塌激励。

Result: 在OpenML-CC18基准的多样化表格数据集上，CoCo达到与核SVM、随机森林、点回归和交叉熵神经网络等SOTA方法竞争的性能。理论和实验均证明CoCo促进了更紧致的类聚类和更快的收敛速度。

Conclusion: CoCo损失函数是一种有效的学习判别性表示的目标函数，在保持竞争性预测性能的同时，实现了更好的表示结构化和收敛效率。

Abstract: In this work, we introduce CoCo, a loss function aimed at learning normalized and well-structured representations. The proposed loss encourages intra-class collapse and inter-class contrast while preserving sufficient flexibility for neural networks to approximate geometrically optimal embeddings with large angular separation between classes. We provide a theoretical analysis positioning CoCo with respect to related objectives such as dot regression and cross-entropy, showing that the new proposed loss benefits from closer initialization to the optimal configuration, more informative gradients, and stronger incentives for class-wise representation collapse. Extensive experiments on diverse tabular datasets from the OpenML-CC18 benchmark show that CoCo achieves competitive performance with state-of-the-art methods, including kernel SVM, Random Forest, dot regression, and cross-entropy-based neural networks. In addition, both theoretical arguments and empirical analyses demonstrate that the proposal promotes tighter class clustering and faster convergence. These results highlight CoCo loss as an effective objective for learning discriminative representations while maintaining competitive predictive performance.

</details>


### [189] [Efficient Sequential Calibration with $O(T^{2/3-ε})$ Error Bound](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12928)
*Zihan Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种高效的随机预测器，通过结合SPR-Calibration程序与外部Blackwell校正层，将在线二值序列校准问题的期望校准误差突破至O(T^{2/3-ε})，超越了经典的T^{2/3}下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 突破在线二值序列校准问题中经典的T^{2/3}校准误差下界，进一步提升校准性能。

Method: 将SPR-Calibration程序与外部Blackwell风格校正层相结合。SPR-Calibration控制关于条件均值估计代理序列的校准误差，校正层控制代理序列与真实结果之间残差异的额外误差。总校准误差分解为代理校准误差和残差，前者由SPR-Calibration保证界，后者通过二次势能论证及SPR-Calibration预测器的稀疏性来控制。

Result: 实现了期望校准误差O(T^{2/3-ε})，其中ε>0为常数。

Conclusion: 通过双层校正结构成功突破了校准误差的经典下界，为在线校准问题提供了更优的理论保证。

Abstract: We study the online binary sequential calibration problem. A recent breakthrough by \citet{dagan2024breaking} overcomes the classical \(T^{2/3}\) barrier for calibration error. Building on this result, we present an efficient randomized forecaster that achieves an expected calibration error \(O(T^{2/3-\varepsilon})\) for some constant \(\varepsilon>0\).
  Our forecaster combines the \textsc{SPR-Calibration} procedure \citep{dagan2024breaking} with an outer Blackwell-style correction layer. The \textsc{SPR-Calibration} procedure controls calibration with respect to a surrogate sequence of conditional-mean estimates, while the correction layer controls the additional error incurred when these surrogates are used to approximate the true outcomes. The analysis decomposes the total calibration error into the surrogate calibration error and the residual discrepancy between the surrogate sequence and the true outcomes. The former is bounded by the \textsc{SPR-Calibration} guarantee in \citet{dagan2024breaking}, and the latter is controlled using a quadratic potential argument together with the sparsity of the \textsc{SPR-Calibration} forecaster.

</details>


### [190] [The Spectrum Is Not Enough: When Context Helps Time-Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13006)
*Mert Onur Cakiroglu,Mehmet Dalkilic,Hasan Kurban*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明基于功率谱的可预测性指标无法衡量上下文（如长回溯、检索增强或预训练模型）带来的价值，因为谱指标对相位随机化不变，而上下文的价值来源于超越二阶统计的非高斯结构。作者提出覆盖率缺陷（coverage deficit）作为无标签的诊断指标，通过七个基准实验验证了该理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究广泛使用基于功率谱的指标（如频谱熵）来衡量时间序列的可预测性，但实践中研究者常将这些指标解读为"添加上下文（长回溯、检索插件、预训练模型）是否会提升预测性能"。然而这两个问题本质不同：谱指标衡量的是序列自身的可预测性，而上下文价值取决于运行点（operating point）和超越二阶统计量的非线性结构。作者旨在厘清这一混淆，并提供可操作的诊断工具。

Method: 1) 理论分析：证明任何基于功率谱的指标在相位随机化下保持不变，而相位随机化后的序列渐近服从高斯分布，因此丢失了超越二阶统计量的信息。2) 构造替身对（surrogate pairs）：固定功率谱和边缘分布，仅破坏相位结构以隔离超越谱的信息。3) 提出覆盖率缺陷（coverage deficit）：一种无标签、配置级别的诊断指标，其主体项通过线性预测与类比预测的增益差来度量超越谱的结构。4) 在七个基准数据集上验证：对窗口键控检索、预训练模型、长线性窗口三种上下文类型进行替身对测试。

Result: 1) 窗口键控检索的价值在替身对上从ECL中位数+33%骤降至-35%（p<10^{-40}），而所有谱指标保持不变。2) 预训练模型的价值分裂为可存活的二阶部分和崩塌的超越线性部分。3) 长线性窗口的价值在替身对上存活。4) 留一数据集交叉验证中，结构项能预测超越谱价值的符号，而谱指标则相反。

Conclusion: 基于功率谱的可预测性指标不能用于判断上下文（检索、预训练模型等）的价值，因为上下文价值来源于超越二阶统计量的非高斯结构。覆盖率缺陷可作为有效的无标签诊断工具，帮助研究者决定是否部署上下文增强方法。本工作的贡献在于概念区分、受控比较和部署决策诊断，而非提出新的预测模型。

Abstract: A growing family of indices scores how predictable a series is from its spectrum. Practitioners increasingly read these scores as answering a different question: whether \emph{adding context}, a longer lookback, a retrieval plug-in, or a pretrained model, will help. These are not the same question. The value of context is a property of the operating point, not of the series. Any index built from the power spectrum is invariant under phase randomization, whereas the beyond-second-order value that retrieval and foundation models supply is not, because a phase-randomized series is asymptotically Gaussian. We state this as an impossibility result and isolate it with surrogate pairs that fix the spectrum and the marginal by construction. We then give a label-free, configuration-level diagnostic, the coverage deficit, whose principal term measures beyond-spectrum structure as the gain of analog over linear prediction. On seven benchmarks the prediction holds: window-keyed retrieval's value collapses across surrogate pairs (ECL median $+33\%\!\to\!-35\%$, $p{<}10^{-40}$) while every spectral index stays frozen; a foundation model's value splits into a surviving second-order part and a small beyond-linear margin that collapses; a longer linear window's value survives. Leave-one-dataset-out, the structure term predicts the sign of beyond-spectrum value where the spectral indices trail it, and the reverse holds for the second-order mechanism. We introduce no new forecaster; the contribution is the distinction, a controlled comparison, and a diagnostic for the deployment decision. Code: https://anonymous.4open.science/r/SINE.

</details>


### [191] [TerraZero: Procedural Driving Simulation for Zero-Demonstration Self-Play at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13028)
*Zhouchonghao Wu,Akshay Rangesh,Weixin Li,Wei-Jer Chang,Zachary Lee,Tim Wang,Wei Zhan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TerraZero是一个程序化驾驶模拟器和自博弈训练框架，通过CPU模拟+GPU零拷贝推理实现每秒130万智能体步数的运算速度，仅使用真实地图几何数据生成无限随机场景，从零开始通过纯强化学习训练策略，在InterPlan长尾基准测试中首个超越所有学习型规划器，在Waymo Open Sim Agents基准上优于其他无演示方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有驾驶模拟器在速度（RL规模化训练所需）、逼真度（反映真实地图结构）和多样性（覆盖安全关键长尾场景）三者之间存在权衡，难以同时满足，且日志数据中罕见的长尾事件难以充分获取。

Method: 采用可配置C引擎，CPU运行模拟、GPU运行策略推理，通过零拷贝路径消除数据传输瓶颈；将日志数据仅作为地图几何信息来源，每张地图通过随机化规则驱动交通参与者、信号控制器、智能体动力学、奖励和尺寸生成无限多样化场景；采用跨GPU的纯强化学习自博弈训练，无人类演示和无推理时回退规划器。

Result: 单服务器GPU上达到1.3M agent-steps/秒；在InterPlan长尾基准测试中成为首个超越所有学习型规划器的全学习策略；在val14常规驾驶基准中取得最佳碰撞和碰撞时间分数；在Waymo Open Sim Agents基准中优于其他无演示方法，与最强参考锚定自博弈方法竞争力相当；策略零样本泛化到不同城市和数据集。

Conclusion: TerraZero证明纯强化学习结合高效程序化模拟器可以同时胜任驾驶策略和模拟智能体两种角色，在速度、逼真度和多样性之间取得平衡，为自动驾驶安全关键长尾场景的学习提供了有效范式。

Abstract: Training robust autonomous driving agents requires a simulator that is fast enough for reinforcement learning at scale, realistic enough to ground behavior in real-world map structure, and diverse enough to cover the safety-critical long tail that logged data rarely contains. We present TerraZero, a procedural driving simulator and self-play training stack. A configurable C engine runs simulation on the CPU and policy inference on the GPU over a zero-copy path, sustaining 1.3M agent-steps per second on a single server-grade GPU, far faster than existing object-level simulators, while keeping fidelity lighter single-agent systems omit: heterogeneous agents, multiple dynamics models, and full traffic-rule enforcement. TerraZero treats logged data only as a source of real-world map geometry, populating each map with randomized rule-based road users and signal controllers and randomizing agent dynamics, rewards, and sizes per episode, so a map yields an unbounded set of scenarios. Every reported policy trains from scratch by reinforcement learning alone on a compute-efficient self-play recipe across GPUs, with zero human demonstrations and no fallback planner at inference. Policies generalize zero-shot across cities and datasets, including emergent left-hand-traffic driving without explicit supervision. As an ego policy, TerraZero is the first fully learned policy to top the InterPlan long-tail benchmark, ahead of larger learned planners; on routine-driving val14 it ranks among the best approaches and is the safest, posting the best collision and time-to-collision scores. On Waymo Open Sim Agents realism the same recipe outperforms other demonstration-free methods and is competitive with the strongest reference-anchored self-play method. One stack serves both roles: driving policies across dynamics for cars and trucks, and sim agents that jointly control vehicles, pedestrians, and cyclists.

</details>


### [192] [The Seriality Gap in Video Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13031)
*Jorge Diaz Chao,Konpat Preechakul,Yuxi Liu,Yutong Bai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 视频扩散模型在多球硬球动力学任务中表现出"序列性差距"：随着因果链增长，模型性能下降，而增加去噪步数无法提供可扩展的序列计算能力，该问题源于去噪过程本质上的结构限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究视频扩散模型在处理需要序列推理的物理模拟任务（如多球碰撞动力学）时的能力边界，特别是因果链长度对模型性能的影响。

Method: 通过多球硬球动力学控制实验，对比标准双向视频扩散模型在不同因果链长度下的表现；设置长度匹配的单球控制实验隔离依赖事件结构的影响；进行干预实验比较自回归/块生成和增加架构深度等方法的效果；最后给出理论证明。

Result: 1) 视频扩散模型在多球碰撞任务中随因果链增长性能下降；2) 单球对照组中退化基本消失，说明是因依赖事件结构而非视频长度导致；3) 增加有效串行计算的方法（自回归/块生成、架构深度）能不成比例地提升性能；4) 理论证明去噪步数无法在确定性视频预测中增加超出骨干网络的串行计算。

Conclusion: 提出"序列性差距"概念：视频扩散模型的去噪循环不能提供可扩展的串行计算，这与其需要串行推理和模拟的任务之间存在结构性不匹配，表明现有视频扩散架构在处理序列推理任务时存在根本性局限。

Abstract: When one ball strikes another, then another, video models should predict the consequences of each bounce. In controlled experiments on multi-ball hard-sphere dynamics, we find that the performance of standard bidirectional video diffusion degrades as the causal chain lengthens, even when provided more denoising steps. In a length-matched single-ball control, where ball-ball interactions are absent, the degradation largely disappears, isolating dependent-event structure rather than video length as the cause. Across intervention studies, methods that increase effective serial computation improve performance disproportionately, including autoregressive/blockwise generation and architectural depth. We identify this pattern as the seriality gap: a mismatch between tasks requiring growing serial computation and video diffusion models whose denoising loop does not provide scalable serial compute. We then prove that, for deterministic video prediction, denoising steps do not add serial computation beyond the backbone, indicating a structural obstacle for video diffusion on serial reasoning and simulation tasks.

</details>


<div id='cs.AR'></div>

# cs.AR [[Back]](#toc)

### [193] [FPGA-Based Mini X-Ray Detector Front-End](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12126)
*Kris Paetow,D. G. Perera*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文简要介绍了医学成像系统中前端电子设备的需求，并提出了一个基于FPGA的小型前端演示方案，用于简化医学成像概念。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学成像系统需要可靠的前端电子设备来采集传感器数据、处理图像信息、识别错误并与系统其他部分通信，而FPGA因其处理大数据量、可预测时序和低延迟特性而适用于此类应用。

Method: 将医学成像概念简化为一个基于FPGA的小型前端演示系统。

Result: 未在摘要中明确给出具体实验结果。

Conclusion: FPGA适用于医学成像和信号处理应用，并可通过简化演示系统展示其前端处理能力。

Abstract: Medical imaging systems require reliable front-end electronics that can acquire sensor data, process image information, identify errors, and communicate results to other parts of the system. In applications such as X-ray imaging, CT, PET, ultrasound, and other diagnostic imaging systems, the electronics must often handle large amounts of data while maintaining predictable timing and low-latency operation. Because of these requirements, FPGAs (Field Programmable Gate Arrays) are commonly useful for medical imaging and signal-processing applications. In this project, the medical imaging concept is simplified into a small FPGA-based frontend demonstration.

</details>


### [194] [ORRAM: An OpenROAD-Integrated RAM Generator Using Standard Cells](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12244)
*Brayden Louie,Thinh P. Nguyen,Matt Liberty,Austin Rovinski*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: ORRAM是一个基于标准单元的存储器生成器，作为OpenROAD的原生模块，无需定制位单元或外部工具即可生成全布局布线的RAM块，支持多种配置，在SkyWater 130nm上实现了与历史DFFRAM相当的位密度，并提供了更丰富的功能集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在交钥匙ASIC设计流程中，从RTL推断触发器会创建大量密集互连的实例，严重拖慢设计流程并影响性能。现有的存储器编译器是第三方工具，通常依赖特定PDK并可能需要基础PDK中不包含的专用单元。ORRAM旨在解决这些缺陷，提供一个无需定制位单元或外部工具的标准单元存储器解决方案。

Method: ORRAM作为OpenROAD的原生模块实现，基于标准单元库生成全布局布线的RAM块。它使用OpenSTA进行时序验证而非SPICE仿真，支持任意字宽、字计数、掩码粒度、多端口读配置、列复用、锁存器存储，并自动进行PDK无关的单元选择。

Result: 在SkyWater 130nm工艺上评估，ORRAM的位密度与历史DFFRAM结果相当，同时提供了显著扩展的功能集。兼容多种标准单元库，包括sky130hd和NanGate45。

Conclusion: ORRAM成功提供了一种基于标准单元的、PDK无关的存储器生成方案，解决了传统方法中第三方工具依赖和定制单元需求的问题，且源代码已作为OpenROAD项目的一部分开源。

Abstract: Memory inference remains a significant challenge in turnkey ASIC design flows. Inferring flip-flops from RTL can create thousands of densely interconnected instances which dramatically slow down design flows and impede performance. Memory compilers address this issue, although they are third-party tools which are often PDK-specific and may require specialized cells not in the base PDK.
  To address these shortcomings, we present ORRAM, a standard-cell-based memory generator built as a native module within OpenROAD. Given a standard cell library, ORRAM produces a fully placed and routed RAM block requiring no custom bitcells or external tooling, with timing verification via OpenSTA rather than SPICE simulation. ORRAM supports arbitrary word sizes, word counts, mask granularities, multi-port read configurations, column muxing, latch-based storage, and automatic PDK-agnostic cell selection, making it compatible with most standard cell libraries including sky130hd and NanGate45. Evaluated on SkyWater 130nm, ORRAM matches the bit density of historical DFFRAM results while offering a significantly expanded feature set. The source code is available as part of the OpenROAD project.

</details>


### [195] [Emulated Integrity Replica: Enabling Self-Healing on FPGA SoCs via Hierarchical Twins](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12298)
*Arsalan Ali Malik,Ali Suvizi,Guru Venkataramani,Aydin Aysu*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出Emulated Integrity Replica (EIR)，一种面向FPGA SoC的分层数字孪生框架，利用处理系统中的空闲周期托管粗粒度（Rabbit）和细粒度（Tortoise）两个互补孪生模型，实现自主故障检测与恢复，在降低能耗和面积开销的同时达到高故障覆盖率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CNN加速器在SoC平台上的容错方案存在瓶颈：硬件冗余（DMR/TMR）资源开销过高，软件方法（ABFT、检查点重启、指令级复制等）引入显著延迟/能耗开销、降低模型精度或对加速器故障覆盖不足。

Method: EIR利用FPGA SoC中可编程逻辑执行加速器时处理系统的空闲周期，部署两个数字孪生模型：Rabbit（粗粒度行为模型，快速故障检测）和Tortoise（细粒度门级模型，基于检查点状态精确恢复），通过周期性捕获加速器状态并利用执行速度画像平衡性能开销与弹性。

Result: 在代表性负载上，EIR相比DMR基线实现了高经验故障覆盖率，同时降低了能耗和面积开销。

Conclusion: EIR为资源受限的边缘AI部署提供了一种实用的弹性路径，在严格控制资源预算的条件下实现了有效的容错能力。

Abstract: Convolutional neural networks (CNNs) are increasingly being deployed on system-on-chip (SoC) platforms, where hardware-accelerated inference enables low-latency edge computing. Achieving fault tolerance on these devices remains challenging because conventional redundancy (dual/triple modular redundancy, DMR/TMR) incurs high resource cost, while software-centric methods (e.g., algorithm-based fault tolerance (ABFT), checkpoint-restart, instruction-level duplication, and software watchdogs/assertions) introduce nontrivial latency/energy overheads, reduce model accuracy, or provide inadequate coverage for accelerator-induced faults.
  In this paper, we propose Emulated Integrity Replica (EIR), a hierarchical digital-twin framework for FPGA SoCs that provides autonomous fault detection and recovery. Unlike DMR/TMR, which replicates hardware logic and incurs proportional area and power overheads, EIR avoids fabric-level duplication by exploiting temporal slack in the processing system (PS). During accelerator execution in the programmable logic (PL), the PS typically remains underutilized; EIR capitalizes on these idle cycles to host two complementary twins: (i) Rabbit: a coarse-grained behavioral model for rapid fault detection and (ii) Tortoise: a fine-grained gate-level model that performs precise recovery from checkpointed states. The accelerator state is captured periodically, leveraging the accelerator's execution-speed profiling to balance performance overhead and resilience. Experiments on representative workloads show that EIR achieves high empirical fault coverage relative to a DMR baseline while reducing energy and area under the evaluated fault model and workload assumptions, indicating a practical path to resilient edge-AI deployments under strict resource budgets.

</details>


### [196] [ArchSim: Computer Architecture Simulation as a Service](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12359)
*Sabila Al Jannat,Wenhan Lyu,Le Khanh Trinh Mai,Huizhi Zhao,Zhuoyan Zheng,Katherine E. Isaacs,Yifan Sun*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: ArchSim是一个将计算机架构模拟研究结构显式化的平台，通过声明式硬件拓扑图自动生成可执行代码、无状态运行器自动调度实验、结构化输出管理，在96个GPU负载配置的矩阵中实现了与手写代码中位误差0.18%、开销仅1.6秒的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统计算机架构模拟研究中，配置、执行和分析被隐式编码在脚本或目录约定中，导致研究难以扩展、难以复现，且每个阶段都依赖定制工具。

Method: 将硬件拓扑描述为声明式图结构，自动生成可执行的模拟代码；采用无状态运行器从共享实验存储中自动领取和执行任务；模拟输出作为结构化工件存储，与配置、基准测试和硬件组件关联。

Result: 在12×8=96个配置的模拟矩阵上，声明式模拟规范驱动的完整模拟中位内核时间误差为0.18%（相对于手写MGPUSim配置），覆盖95.8%的配置；每个模拟仅引入1.6秒的开销。

Conclusion: ArchSim通过显式化模拟研究结构，解决了传统方法在可扩展性、可复现性和工具依赖方面的问题，同时保持了高精度和低开销。

Abstract: Conducting a complete computer architecture simulation study is challenging because configuration, execution, and analysis are often encoded implicitly in scripts or directory conventions rather than represented explicitly. As a result, studies are difficult to scale, hard to reproduce, and dependent on custom tooling at every stage. We present ArchSim, which makes the structure of a simulation study explicit. In ArchSim, hardware topologies are described as declarative graphs that automatically generate executable simulation code, eliminating hand-written simulator programs. Stateless runners autonomously claim and execute jobs from a shared experiment store, enabling configuration-benchmark matrices to scale without manual orchestration. Simulation outputs are stored as structured artifacts tied to configurations, benchmarks, and hardware components, enabling systematic result exploration without custom parsers. We evaluate ArchSim on a 12 x 8 = 96-configuration simulation matrix spanning memory-bound, compute-bound, and mixed-intensity GPU workloads. Declarative simulation specifications drive full simulations with a median kernel time error of 0.18% relative to hand-written MGPUSim configurations across 95.8% of configurations. The platform introduces only 1.6 seconds of overhead per simulation, negligible relative to realistic simulation workloads.

</details>


### [197] [Realizable N:M Sparse Transformer Inference via Search-Kernel Co-Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12505)
*Yiming Liu,Wenqi Lou,Zhiguang Wang,Zhiwei Ke,Fengrui Zuo,Chao Wang,Xuehai Zhou*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向N:M稀疏ViT推理的软硬件协同设计框架，硬件端设计MD-SpMM CUDA内核将稀疏GEMM重组为微密集的张量核心对齐数据流，软件端采用三阶段启发式搜索进行逐层稀疏度分配，在多种ViT/Swin模型和GPU平台上实现超过2.2倍延迟加速，同时保持可比的精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 半结构化N:M稀疏在理论上能降低算术成本，但在现代GPU上往往无法转化为同比例端到端加速，原因是部署延迟不仅取决于算术减少量，还受稀疏条件下的执行规律性和硬件调度影响。因此需要稀疏执行与稀疏配置的协同设计来实现实际加速。

Method: 硬件层面设计MD-SpMM内核，将稀疏GEMM重组为微密集的Tensor Core对齐数据流，并采用推理感知的自适应并行度以维持利用率；软件层面进行逐层稀疏度搜索，采用三阶段启发式搜索结合约束松弛，在显式的端到端延迟预算下分配稀疏度，避免过早收敛。

Result: 在多个ViT/Swin模型和GPU平台上，框架实现超过2.2倍延迟加速，同时保持可比精度；在相同延迟约束下能实现更高的精度。

Conclusion: 通过硬件-软件协同设计，能够有效弥合N:M稀疏理论加速与实际加速之间的差距，实现显著的端到端推理加速。

Abstract: Vision Transformers (ViTs) achieve strong accuracy but incur high inference latency. Semi-structured N:M sparsity can reduce arithmetic cost, yet its theoretical savings often fail to translate into proportional end-to-end speedups on modern GPUs. This mismatch arises because deployment latency depends not only on arithmetic reduction but also on execution regularity and hardware scheduling under sparsity. Achieving practical acceleration, therefore, requires coordinated design across sparse execution and sparsity configuration. To this end, we propose a hardware-software co-design framework for N:M sparse ViT inference. On the hardware side, we design MD-SpMM, an N:M sparse CUDA kernel that reorganizes sparse GEMM into micro-dense, Tensor-Core-aligned dataflow and uses inference-aware adaptive parallelism to sustain utilization. On the software side, we perform layer-wise sparsity search under explicit end-to-end latency budgets using a three-stage heuristic search with constraint relaxation to avoid premature convergence and enable deployment-aware sparsity allocation. Experiments on multiple ViT/Swin models and GPU platforms show that the framework achieves over 2.2x latency speedup while maintaining comparable accuracy and delivering superior accuracy under the same latency constraint. The source code is publicly available at https://github.com/liuganhuo/realizable-nm-sparse-transformer.

</details>


### [198] [CLIP-3D: Closed-Loop Evaluation of Performance and Physical Constraints for 3D ICs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12788)
*Shuo Ren,Libo Shen,Yaohui Han,Leilei Jin,Chenghan Wang,Zhen Zhuang,Rongliang Fu,Bei Yu,Tsung-Yi Ho*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: CLIP-3D 是一个面向早期 3D-IC 架构探索的 shift-left 流程，通过集成 McPAT、CACTI、HotSpot 和解析热感知布局规划器，将 3D 布局驱动的热、互连和缓存效应引入架构级模拟之前，直接以 realized BIPS 为优化目标而非传统 HPWL+温度替代指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统架构模拟器（如 gem5）在理想化时序下报告 IPC，不产生每模块功耗图、缓存周期数或 3D 布局信息，因此早期 3D-IC 探索无法考虑决定芯片实际是否限流的布局效应（热、互连、缓存几何），导致设计选择不准确。

Method: 两阶段流程：第一阶段将架构配置提升为物理块表示（McPAT 用于动态/泄漏功耗、CACTI 用于缓存几何与访问周期、HotSpot 兼容的 3D 堆叠离散化）；第二阶段运行解析热感知 3D 布局规划器，目标函数嵌入基于 HotSpot 稳态算子线性性和 CMOS 功耗-频率分解导出的闭式持续频率表达式，跨层宏分配与面内布局协同优化 realized BIPS。

Result: 所提出的流程能够在调用任何 sign-off 工具之前，将 3D 布局驱动的热、互连和缓存效应暴露给早期架构探索，实现以 realized BIPS 为直接优化目标的跨层宏分配与面内布局协同优化。

Conclusion: CLIP-3D 提供了一种 shift-left 方法，使 3D-IC 早期架构探索能够纳入布局级物理效应（热、线长、缓存时序），并直接优化实际吞吐量（BIPS）而非代理指标。

Abstract: 3D integration packs more power into a smaller footprint, so a candidate design's actual throughput depends on its layout: which macro sits on which tier, where the hot spot lands, and how cache geometry maps to access cycles. Architectural simulators like gem5 report IPC under idealized timing. They do not produce the per-block power map, the cache cycle counts, or the 3D layout that decide the realized billion-instructions-per-second (BIPS), so early-stage 3D-IC exploration selects designs without accounting for the effects that decide whether they throttle on silicon. We present CLIP-3D, a shift-left flow that exposes 3D layout-driven thermal, wire, and cache effects to early-stage architectural exploration before any sign-off tool is invoked. The first stage lifts an architectural configuration into a physical block representation: McPAT for per-block dynamic and leakage power, CACTI for cache geometry and access cycles, and a HotSpot-compatible 3D stack discretization. The second stage runs an analytical 3D thermal-aware floorplanner over that representation. The floorplanner objective embeds a closed-form sustained-frequency expression derived from the linearity of HotSpot's steady-state operator and the standard CMOS power-frequency decomposition. Cross-tier macro assignment and in-plane placement are co-optimized for the realized BIPS rather than for a half-perimeter wirelength (HPWL)-plus-temperature surrogate with hand-tuned weights.

</details>


### [199] [A 32-channel event-based bio-signal analog front-end with adaptive delta and pulse frequency encoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12901)
*Narayanan Shyam,Saptarshi Ghosh,Giacomo Indiveri*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一款32通道、低功耗、基于事件的模拟前端专用集成电路（ASIC），用于生物医学信号采集与编码，支持脉冲频率调制（PFM）和自适应异步Delta调制器（aADM）双模式编码，具备自动缩放机制实现高数据压缩，采用180 nm CMOS工艺制造，兼容脉冲神经网络处理器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低功耗、基于事件的模拟前端是实现高效端到端神经形态信号处理系统的关键，现有方案在数据压缩和自适应编码方面存在不足，难以满足在线处理脑机接口信号的需求。

Method: 设计了一款32通道独立可编程的AFE ASIC，包含PFM和aADM双模式编码机制；aADM编码器利用自动缩放机制，根据输入信号包络实时调整编码数据率，实现高数据压缩。

Result: 采用180 nm CMOS工艺流片，实现高度可配置的接口，兼容现有SNN神经形态处理器，在数据压缩和低功耗方面具有显著优势。

Conclusion: 该工作为脑机接口中神经信号的在线处理与自适应无线通信提供了可行的低功耗解决方案，推动了端到端神经形态信号处理系统的发展。

Abstract: Low-power event-based Analog Front-Ends (AFEs) are essential for building efficient, end-to-end neuromorphic signal processing systems. In this paper, we present an event-based AFE Application-Specific Integrated Circuit (ASIC) optimized for biomedical signal acquisition and encoding. The chip features 32 independently programmable input channels with dual-mode encoding mechanism outputs, comprising Pulse Frequency Modulation (PFM) and adaptive Asynchronous Delta Modulator (aADM) circuits. The aADM encoder provides an auto-scaling mechanism that adapts the encoding data-rate based on the input signal envelope in real-time, enabling very high data compression for low-power information transmission. This approach paves the way toward adaptive wireless communication of neural signals for on-line processing in brain-computer interfaces. Fabricated in a 180 nm CMOS process, the proposed ASIC offers a highly configurable interface compatible with state-of-the-art Spiking Neural Network (SNN) neuromorphic processors.

</details>
